Specifically, we evaluate how susceptible LLMs are to direct and suggestive prompts that encourage models to support or challenge particular positions.
  We evaluate six LLMs using 160 distinct prompts spanning ten topics across opinion-based and factual domains. The prompts systematically vary in prompting strategy, support versus challenge instructions, prompt polarity, users' expressed beliefs, and topic domain, spanning both opinion-based and factual questions. Our results show that LLMs systematically adapt their responses to align with prompt framing, even in factual contexts. This suggests that prompt framing can outweigh factual consistency in model responses. Overall, our findings delineate the extent and boundaries of LLM manipulability. Furthermore, the results imply that LLMs can reinforce subtle user biases and are susceptible to explicit prompt manipulation even in domains where responses should remain factually stable.

</details>


### [35] [GPTKB 2.0: Browsing, Querying, and Auditing a Disambiguated LLM-Derived Knowledge Base](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06992)
*Yujia Hu,Tuan-Phong Nguyen,Simon Razniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍GPTKB 2.0，一个从大型语言模型物化的大规模消歧知识库，包含3840万三元组、160万规范实体、20.76万关系及6.6万类别，并提供交互式网页演示支持浏览、溯源审计、SPARQL查询、自然语言问答和实体链接等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM派生知识库大多基于表面字符串识别实体，缺乏消歧能力，导致同义词无法合并、同形异义词无法区分，限制了知识库的质量和可用性。

Method: GPTKB 2.0采用上下文引导的消歧机制，在递归知识库构建过程中分离同形异义词并合并同义提及；同时构建交互式网页演示，支持实体浏览、事实溯源审计（包括表面形式、候选匹配、源三元组和消歧决策）、结构化SPARQL查询、自然语言转SPARQL以及文本实体链接。

Result: GPTKB 2.0包含3840万三元组、160万规范实体、20.76万关系和6.6万类别；提供完整可下载的知识库及在线演示平台（https://gptkb.org/）。

Conclusion: GPTKB 2.0通过上下文引导的消歧策略显著提升了LLM派生知识库的实体规范化能力，并使构建过程透明可审计，为大规模知识库的探索与验证提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We present a web demo for exploring a large-scale disambiguated knowledge base (KB) materialized from a large language model (LLM). GPTKB 2.0 contains 38.4M triples over 1.6M canonical entities, together with 207.6K consolidated relations and 66K consolidated classes. Unlike prior LLM-derived knowledge bases that largely identify entities by surface strings, GPTKB 2.0 performs context-guided disambiguation during recursive KB construction, separating homonyms and merging synonymous mentions as facts are elicited. The demo makes this process inspectable: users can browse entities, follow links across the KB, and audit the provenance of individual facts, including surface forms, candidate matches, source triples, and disambiguation decisions. The interface further supports structured SPARQL queries, natural-language questions translated to SPARQL, and entity linking from user-provided text to canonical GPTKB 2.0 entries. GPTKB 2.0 is available at https://gptkb.org/, with the full KB downloadable for offline use.

</details>


### [36] [Does More Retrieved Evidence Help Visual Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Diffusion Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07006)
*Jiankun Wang,Yisen Gao,Ziwei Zhang,Xingcheng Fu,Jiaxin Bai,Chen Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视觉检索增强生成（RAG）中"无条件将所有检索到的证据传递给生成器"这一隐含假设在扩散语言模型（DLMs）中不成立的问题，并提出了基于熵的候选过滤器（ECF），一种无需训练的、通过选择性证据准入来保留检索覆盖率同时限制有害视觉暴露的框架，在多个基准上显著提升答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉RAG假设检索到的所有证据都应传递给生成器，但作者发现对于扩散语言模型，无条件传递所有检索页面反而会因语义冲突降低答案准确率。论文通过潜在源分析揭示这一矛盾源于并行去噪中的源一致性损失，因此需要更精细的证据选择机制。

Method: 提出Entropy-Based Candidate Filter（ECF），一个无需训练的框架：(1) 构建多粒度证据单元以减少单个候选内的无关内容；(2) 使用空白对照的块置信度和检索排名来判断候选是否应进入最终上下文，从而在解码前评估证据质量。其关键洞察是干扰在第一步答案块分布中已可见，因此可以在解码前进行证据评估。

Result: 在三个多模态DLM和五个视觉QA基准上，ECF相比最强的固定top-k输入平均提升2.62个百分点准确率；基于LLaDA2.0-Uni时，相比每个数据集上最佳的无需训练的竞争方法平均提升2.37个百分点。

Conclusion: 更广泛的检索对视觉DLM-RAG的效益应通过选择性证据准入而非无条件证据扩展来实现。这一发现挑战了传统RAG中"证据越多越好"的普遍假设，并为扩散模型背景下的检索增强生成提供了新的设计原则。

Abstract: Visual retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) commonly expands the retrieved evidence set to improve answer-page coverage, implicitly assuming that all available evidence should be passed to the generator. We show that this assumption does not hold for diffusion language models (DLMs): retrieving more pages increases answer-page recall, whereas unconditionally passing all retrieved pages to the generator often reduces answer accuracy, primarily because of semantic conflict. A latent-source analysis explains this mismatch through source-coherence loss in parallel denoising, where position-wise proposals can combine incompatible visual sources into unsupported answers. We further find that such interference is already visible in the first-step answer-block distribution, making it possible to assess evidence before decoding. To preserve retrieval coverage while limiting harmful visual exposure, we propose the Entropy-Based Candidate Filter (ECF), a training-free evidence-admission framework. To reduce irrelevant content within individual candidates, ECF constructs multi-granularity evidence units; to identify beneficial additional evidence, it uses blank-controlled block confidence and retrieval rank to determine whether and which candidate should enter the final context. Across three multimodal DLMs and five visual QA benchmarks, ECF improves answer accuracy by 2.62 percentage points on average over the strongest fixed top-$k$ input and, with LLaDA2.0-Uni, by 2.37 percentage points on average over the best competing training-free result for each dataset. These results show that broader retrieval benefits visual DLM-RAG through selective evidence admission rather than unconditional evidence expansion. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/wjkuser/ECF.

</details>

Result: 该系统能够自主适应快速演变且嘈杂的技能提及，覆盖五种欧洲语言，提供了一个高度可扩展、可解释且自愈的技能知识图谱生成框架，并从中推导出结构化分类体系。

Conclusion: 该管道为从非结构化、嘈杂文本中生成全面的技能知识图谱提供了有效的解决方案，在保持知识图谱实体稳定性的同时，具备动态扩展新概念的能力，兼顾了可扩展性、可解释性和自愈性。

Abstract: Organizing thousands of unstandardized, multilingual expertise declarations is a persistent challenge for Human Resources (HR) platforms, directly impacting downstream tasks like accurate talent matching. To address this, we propose a hybrid knowledge graph generation pipeline that grounds a Large Language Model (LLM) in the Wikidata multilingual Knowledge Graph (KG) while employing an agentic reflexion pattern to synthesize emerging concepts and their associated metadata. Unlike rigid top-down methods or fragmented bottom-up approaches, our system anchors recognized concepts to stable Knowledge Graph entities while dynamically creating new nodes and relational metadata for unrecognized skills. Executed across five stages, entity reconciliation, multilingual canonicalization, active curation, deduplication, and the iterative recovery of unmapped concepts, the system autonomously adapts to rapidly evolving, noisy skill mentions across five European languages. Ultimately, this pipeline provides a highly scalable, explicable, and self-healing framework for generating a comprehensive skills knowledge graph, from which a structured taxonomy is derived, using unstructured, noisy text.

</details>

*Zhenchao Wang,Xin Chen,Luoxi Zhang,Min Yang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HNR-DAC两阶段框架用于科学声明验证任务，通过硬负样本重排序（HNR）量化证据混淆度，以及分布对齐分类（DAC）使训练与推理分布一致，在NLPCC 2026 Task 10 Track 2上取得了第三名的成绩，并获得了最高的Macro-F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学声明验证面临两个关联挑战：论文内部干扰项常常看起来像真实证据，而基于黄金证据训练的分类器在推理时必须处理检索到的证据，导致训练与推理分布不匹配。

Method: 提出HNR-DAC两阶段框架：(1) 硬负样本重排序（HNR）利用基础重排序器对非黄金段落的评分来量化证据混淆度，将黄金证据与最难区分的候选进行对比；(2) 分布对齐分类（DAC）使用冻结的HNR产生的Top-1段落进行训练，使训练分布与推理输入一致，同时HNR的Top-3段落标识符提供证据输出。

Result: 在NLPCC 2026 Task 10 Track 2上，最终配置取得97.21% Hit@3、95.79% Macro-F1、94.47% Joint@3和95.13%的平均分。官方排行榜排名第三，同时取得最高的93.05% Macro-F1，以及70.16% Joint@3和81.61%的平均分。

Conclusion: 通过在训练阶段对齐推理时实际遇到的分布（DAC）并利用硬负样本增强区分能力（HNR），有效解决了证据混淆和分布偏移问题，验证了该框架的实用性。

Abstract: Scientific claim verification over a cited paper requires predicting the claim--paper relation and identifying the paragraphs that justify that prediction. This setting poses two linked challenges: within-paper distractors often resemble genuine evidence, while a classifier trained on gold evidence must operate on retrieved evidence at inference. We present HNR-DAC, a two-stage framework that trains each stage on the cases it will actually encounter. Hard-Negative Reranking (HNR) quantifies evidence confusability using a base reranker's scores on non-gold paragraphs and contrasts gold evidence against the most confusable candidates. Distribution-Aligned Classification (DAC) trains on the Top-1 paragraph produced by the same frozen HNR used to construct inference inputs, while HNR's Top-3 paragraph identifiers provide the evidence output. On the NLPCC 2026 Task 10 Track 2, the final configuration obtains 97.21% Hit@3, 95.79% Macro-F1, 94.47% Joint@3, and an average score of 95.13%. The corresponding submission ranks third on the official Track 2 leaderboard while achieving the highest overall Macro-F1 of 93.05%, alongside 70.16% Joint@3 and an average score of 81.61%.

</details>


### [39] [From Test-Time Scaling to Reusable Memory: Measuring Crystallization in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07213)
*Jiaqian Wang,Yutao Qi,Wenjin Hou,Yuanxi Che,Muning Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"结晶问题"（crystallization problem），即衡量测试时计算（如文本到SQL的修复）带来的未来价值。通过在BIRD数据集上进行受控实验，发现存储已验证的修正查询可使未见查询的首次尝试准确率提升4.34个百分点，捕获了按需修复44.4%的准确率余量，并识别出数据库特定内容是主要增益来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到SQL系统在测试时通过额外计算修复困难查询，但这些计算在每次回答后被丢弃。虽然系统开始保留已验证的修复经验，但评估仍只报告单一的端到端分数，无法区分对重复问题的回放帮助与对未见问题的帮助，也无法识别负责的记忆选择。因此需要一种方法衡量这种"未来价值"。

Method: 采用受控评估方法：固定单次求解器不变，每次只改变一个记忆组件。分别测量三个维度：回放（replay）、跨问题保留（cross-question retention）、以及保留数据集内的未见数据库转移（held-out same-database transfer）。

Result: 在BIRD数据集上，存储已验证的修正查询使未见查询的首次尝试准确率提升4.34个百分点，占按需修复在同问题上提供准确率余量的44.4%。受控干预发现数据库特定内容是主要操作要素；可靠的验证和更广泛的检索覆盖带来有支撑的增益，而更丰富的格式和复杂的检索器并无额外帮助。

Conclusion: 测试时修复经验可以结晶为对未来查询有价值的记忆，其核心增益来自数据库特定内容。评估应区分回放与新问题上的帮助，本文提供开源代码和复现材料以促进该方向的进一步研究。

Abstract: Test-time scaling can correct difficult text-to-SQL queries, but the extra computation is normally discarded after each answer. Systems increasingly retain verified repair episodes, yet evaluations still report one end-to-end score. It cannot distinguish replay on recurring questions from help on unseen questions, or identify the responsible memory choice. We call measuring this future value the crystallization problem. Our controlled evaluation holds the single-shot solver fixed and varies one memory choice at a time. We separately measure replay, cross-question retention, and held-out same-database transfer. On BIRD, storing verified corrected queries improves held-out first-attempt accuracy by 4.34 percentage points. This gain captures 44.4% of the accuracy headroom provided by on-demand repair on the same questions. Controlled interventions identify database-specific content as the main operating ingredient. Reliable verification and broader retrieval coverage yield supported gains; richer formats and elaborate retrievers do not. Open-source code, evaluation artifacts, and reproduction instructions are available at https://github.com/ai-jiaqian/text-to-sql-memory-crystallization.

</details>


### [40] [Skaling: Chinchilla's Exponents Meet Kaplan's Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07222)
*Mathurin Videau,Badr Youbi-Idrissi,David Lopez-Paz,Kartik Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Skaling law，一种耦合模型容量与数据规模的新颖神经缩放定律函数形式，通过单一交互指数解决传统缩放定律在数据稀缺和过度训练极端情况下的系统性误差，将MAPE降低1.5-3倍，并配合稀疏网格策略节省约10倍计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经缩放定律假设模型大小和训练数据对损失的影响相互独立，这导致在数据稀缺和过度训练两个极端场景下出现系统性高估和低估问题。作者旨在修正这一根本性缺陷，使缩放定律在更广泛的计算预算范围内保持可靠。

Method: 引入Skaling law，一种广义函数形式，通过单一交互指数将模型容量与数据规模耦合起来。同时提出稀疏网格策略，仅在低计算资源区域进行实验，即可实现全网格外推预测。

Result: Skaling law在插值和外推两种场景下均将MAPE降低1.5-3倍；配合稀疏网格策略，仅用约1/10的计算量即可实现与传统均匀扫描相当的准确全网格外推效果。

Conclusion: Skaling law为下一代模型训练的计算预算分配提供了更稳健且资源高效的框架，使小型实验的可靠性能预测成为可能。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws are foundational for language model development, yet standard formulations systematically under- and overestimate loss at data-scarce and overtraining extremes. This failure originates in the underlying assumption that model size and training data impact the loss independently. To address this, we introduce the Skaling law, a generalized functional form that couples model capacity and data through a single interaction exponent. This simple extension reduces the Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) by 1.5-3x across both interpolation and extrapolation regimes. When paired with a sparse grid strategy restricted to low-compute regimes, the Skaling law achieves accurate full-grid extrapolation using approximately 10x less compute than uniform sweeps. By enabling reliable performance prediction from small-scale experiments, the Skaling law provides a more robust and resource-efficient framework for allocating compute budgets in next-generation model training.

</details>


### [41] [Stoicheia: Character-Level Masked Diffusion for Ancient Greek Textual Restoration, Parsing, and Metrical Scansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07249)
*Eric Cullhed,Albin Thörn Cleland*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Stoicheia，一个405M参数的字符级掩码扩散编码器，专门用于古希腊语。其输入分解为五个可独立掩码的平行平面（字母、词句边界、变音符号、大小写、标点），单个骨干网络即可完成文本修复、重新分词、重音标注和标点恢复等任务，无需针对特定任务重新分词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古希腊语文本（尤其是铭文和纸莎草文献）常因损坏缺失大量字符，且原文缺乏分词、大小写和标点，导致下游处理困难。已有模型（如Ithaca）虽在修复方面取得进展，但通常针对单一任务优化，缺乏统一、灵活且可验证的预训练框架。作者希望通过字符级扩散预训练，在一个模型内同时解决多种文本恢复与标注任务。

Method: Stoicheia将输入分解为五个可独立掩码的平面（letters, word/sentence boundaries, diacritics, capitalization, punctuation），采用字符级掩码扩散编码器。预训练于开放的380M词修订固定语料库，发布11个检查点：10个旋转去污染折叠（保证任意文学段落至少有一个未见过该文本的模型），1个不接触公文文本的模型。实验采用随机初始化对照组以隔离扩散预训练的贡献。

Result: 扩散预训练带来了显著提升：铭文/纸莎草重建CER降低5.6点，依存句法分析LAS提升12.9点，梵文度量标注平衡准确率提升6.0点。在Ithaca测试集上，Stoicheia将CER从24.6（Ithaca）和23.5（Aeneas-framework版）降至15.5，top-1准确率从63.0/64.0提升至74.5，超越两个先前最优系统。

Conclusion: Stoicheia证明字符级掩码扩散预训练是古希腊语文本处理的有效统一范式，在多个任务上均超越先前最先进系统，同时通过去污染折叠设计保障了评估的公正性和模型的可验证性。

Abstract: We introduce Stoicheia, a 405M-parameter character-level masked-diffusion encoder for Ancient Greek whose input factors into five aligned, independently maskable planes: letters, word and sentence boundaries, diacritics, capitalization, and punctuation. A single backbone can therefore restore lacunae, re-segment, accentuate, and punctuate unspaced text without task-specific retokenization. We pretrain it on an open, revision-pinned corpus of 380M words and release eleven checkpoints: ten rotated, decontaminated folds, guaranteeing that for any given literary passage at least one released model has never seen its text, and one with no exposure to documentary texts. Three experiments - reconstruction of damaged inscriptions and papyri, morphosyntactic tagging and dependency parsing, and macronization with metrical scansion - each carry a matched random-initialization control, isolating what character-level diffusion pretraining contributes: 5.6 CER points on inscription reconstruction, 12.9 LAS on parsing, and 6.0 points of balanced accuracy on macronization. On Ithaca's own test split, with identical frozen samples and strict scoring, Stoicheia reduces character error relative to both prior state-of-the-art systems, from 24.6 (Ithaca) and 23.5 (its 2025 Aeneas-framework successor) to 15.5, and raises top-1 accuracy from 63.0 and 64.0 to 74.5.

</details>


### [42] [Why Knowing Both Hops Is Not Enough: Understanding Two-Hop Generalization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07261)
*Zili Zhang,Yilin Wang,Heng Wang,Herun Wan,Minnan Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控符号环境从头训练Transformer，揭示了LLM在双跳推理任务上的泛化失败机制：当第二跳符合训练分布时模型表现良好，偏离分布时则必然失败。机理分析表明，成功泛化依赖于跨上下文一致的中问表示涌现，而失败源于下层正确构建中问表示、上层却将其映射为输出而非进行推理的不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为什么LLM能解决复杂多跳问题却在简单的两跳查询上出现令人困惑的失败，为解释这种泛化失败提供内部机制层面的完整解释。

Method: 在受控符号环境中从头训练Transformer，通过机理分析（mechanistic analysis）追踪模型内部表示，并提出循环式训练策略（recurrent-style training strategy）使Transformer复用其推理回路。

Result: 发现双跳泛化模式：第二跳符合训练分布时模型可靠泛化，偏离时总是失败；提出循环式训练策略显著改善分布外双跳查询的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 双跳推理失败的根源是上下层之间的表示不匹配：下层能正确构建中问表示，但上层倾向于将这些表示映射为输出而非进行推理。循环式训练策略可有效缓解此问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can solve complex multi-hop problems yet exhibit puzzling failures on simple two-hop queries: although a model may correctly store each individual hop, it often fails to combine them. To understand the internal mechanisms of this phenomenon, we train transformers from scratch in a controlled symbolic environment. Our experiments reveal a pattern in two-hop generalization: models generalize reliably when the second hop follows the training distribution, but always fail when it deviates.
  Through mechanistic analysis, we provide a complete explanation for these distinct generalization behaviors: in settings where models generalize successfully, performance is driven by the emergence of consistent intermediate representations for the same entities across contexts, whereas failures on settings where the second hop is out-of-distribution arise from a mismatch across layers: lower layers correctly construct these intermediate representations, but upper layers, while trained on corresponding atomic facts, primarily learn to map them to outputs rather than to reason over them.
  Driven by this insight, we propose a recurrent-style training strategy, which enables transformers to reuse their reasoning circuitry across input forms and substantially improves generalization on out-of-distribution two-hop queries.

</details>


### [43] [Gaze Behavior in Visual World Experiments Can be Modeled With Off-the-shelf Language-Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07282)
*Rahul Murali Shankar,Titus von der Malsburg,Sebastian Padó*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的方法，结合CLIP系列的多模态双编码器模型与双模态归因方法，来预测视觉世界实验中的注视行为。该方法无需生成架构和微调，就能稳健地复现一项具有里程碑意义的英语视觉世界研究结果，该研究表明人类具有预测性加工能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于神经语言模型是否可作为人类语言处理模型的研究几乎都集中在单模态（书面或口头语言）场景，而忽略了像视觉世界研究这样的多模态实验范式（同时呈现视觉和语言输入）。作者希望填补这一空白，探索多模态模型在模拟人类语言加工方面的潜力。

Method: 将CLIP家族的多模态双编码器模型与一种双模态归因方法相结合。该模型接受视觉和语言输入，通过归因方法预测视觉世界研究中的人类注视行为，且不需要生成式架构或微调。

Result: 该方法能够稳健地复现一项开创性的英语视觉世界研究的结果，该研究展示了人类的预测性加工行为，且模型并未专门为此任务训练。

Conclusion: 即使没有生成式架构、无需微调且未针对该任务训练，CLIP类的多模态双编码器模型结合双模态归因方法也足以复现视觉世界研究中的人类预测性注视行为，表明此类模型可能作为人类多模态语言处理的合理模型。

Abstract: The recent advances in neural language models have also spurred much work in computational psycholinguistics, asking whether neural LMs are also promising models of human language processing. However, work has been overwhelmingly focused on the unimodal case of written or spoken language. In contrast, multimodal experimental paradigms, like visual world studies that present participants with both visual and linguistic input simultaneously, have been neglected. In this paper, we present a novel approach that predicts gaze behavior in visual world studies. It does so by combining a simple multi-modal bi-encoder model of the CLIP family with a bimodal attribution method. We demonstrate the ability of this approach to robustly replicate the results of a seminal English visual world study which shows hu- man predictive processing. Remarkably, it does so without a generative architecture and without the need for fine-tuning, despite not being trained for this task.

</details>


### [44] [Grammar Engineering Meets LLMs: Development of Cantonese and Irish ParGram Treebanks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07283)
*Chit-Fung Lam,Elaine Uí Dhonnchadha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在ParGram（平行语法）项目中开发粤语和爱尔兰语树库的工作，并探讨了使用多语言大语言模型（LLM）辅助语法工程的潜力与局限。实验表明，LLM在粤语-爱尔兰语翻译上表现不佳且不受提示语言影响；在句法结构生成方面虽能产生部分有意义的输出，但在跨语言抽象任务上表现薄弱。尽管如此，LLM输出仍可为替代分析和谓词-论元关系捕捉提供参考，但专家驱动分析与验证仍然不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语法工程需要语言形式主义与计算实现的专业知识，尤其在平行语法项目中需兼顾跨语言一致性与语言特有属性。作者希望通过评估多语言LLM在语法工程中的辅助能力，探索其方法论的潜力与局限性，以减轻人工语法工程的负担。

Method: 在ParGram项目框架下开发粤语和爱尔兰语树库，保持抽象功能层面的语言平行性；同时使用OpenAI的gpt-oss-120b模型进行粤语-爱尔兰语翻译测试及形式句法结构生成实验，评估不同提示语言和跨语言抽象任务的表现。

Result: 翻译性能整体不理想且不受提示语言影响；句法结构生成方面模型产出部分结构上有意义的输出，但在需要跨语言抽象的任务上表现较差。LLM输出仍有一定参考价值，可提供替代分析并部分捕捉谓词-论元关系。

Conclusion: 研究发现LLM在协作式语法工程中兼具潜力与局限，强调专家驱动的分析与验证在语法工程中仍然具有不可替代的重要性。

Abstract: Grammar engineering requires expertise in linguistic formalism and computational implementation, especially in parallel grammar projects that balance cross-linguistic consistency with language-specific properties. This paper presents the development of Cantonese and Irish treebanks within the Parallel Grammar (ParGram) Project, where linguistic parallelism is maintained at an abstract functional level. We also investigate the methodological potential and limitations of using multilingual LLMs to support grammar engineering, focusing on Cantonese-Irish translation and the generation of formal syntactic structures using OpenAI's gpt-oss-120b model. The results show that translation performance was generally unsatisfactory and unaffected by prompt language. For syntactic structure generation, the model produced some structurally meaningful outputs, but performed poorly on tasks requiring cross-linguistic abstraction. Nonetheless, LLM-generated outputs may still offer some reference value by suggesting alternative analyses and (partially) capturing predicate-argument relations. Overall, our findings highlight both the potential and limitations of using LLMs in collaborative grammar engineering, while underscoring the continued importance of expert-driven analysis and verification.

</details>

*Edoardo Sebastiano De Duro,Emma Franchino,Massimo Stella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"NLP心理测量学"框架，将心理预测视为心理测量问题，利用九个LLM在受控人格条件下完成心理问卷并生成文本解释，结合情绪特征、句法语义网络（forma mentis networks）及人格人口学变量，通过随机森林回归和SHAP解释，在生活满意度（SWLS，70.8%方差解释）、抑郁（PHQ-9，55.7%）和DASS-21各维度（抑郁68.5%、焦虑76.0%、压力72.4%）上取得显著预测效果，并验证了模型在小样本和真实转录数据上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP心理健康预测模型没有明确说明其测量对象（语境知识、情绪内容还是句法结构），缺乏可解释性和可测试性。作者希望将心理学中的心理测量学原则引入NLP，使预测与可解释的语言学证据挂钩，并测试超出训练文本格式的泛化能力。

Method: 使用九个LLM在受控"认知数字影子"人格条件下完成心理问卷（SWLS、PHQ-9、DASS-21）并生成逐项文本解释；从解释文本中提取情绪画像和句法语义网络（forma mentis networks）特征，结合人格与人口学变量输入随机森林回归模型；使用SHAP分析特征贡献方向；通过消融实验、跨域日记预测和行为临床转录分类验证泛化性。

Result: 完整随机森林模型解释SWLS方差70.8%、PHQ-9方差55.7%、DASS-21抑郁68.5%、焦虑76.0%、压力72.4%。仅人口学变量对抑郁/焦虑/压力无意义预测，但对生活满意度有效；神经质和网络拓扑主导抑郁和焦虑预测且方向相反。无需重新训练即可从日记区分低/高评分人格（r达0.91），仅用网络/情绪特征对真实临床转录分类准确率达68%。

Conclusion: LLM人设可以暴露模型偏差、还原与临床反刍一致的模式，并支持无匹配问卷的人类文本心理测量预测；但合成数据不能替代人类验证。NLP心理测量学通过可解释AI和网络/情绪特征使这些区分显式化、可测量和可测试。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) models predicting mental health outcomes rarely specify what they measure: contextual knowledge, emotional content, or syntactic structure. NLP Psychometrics treats psychological prediction from text as a psychometric problem, linking scores to interpretable linguistic evidence and testing beyond the training text format. Nine LLMs, conditioned on controlled personas (cognitive digital shadows), completed psychometric questionnaires with textual explanations per item. We extracted emotional profiles and syntactic-semantic structure via textual forma mentis networks, combined with personality and sociodemographic variables in ablated random forest (RF) regressors, using SHAP to identify which features drove performance and in which direction. Full RF models explained up to 70.8% of variance in life satisfaction (SWLS), 55.7% in depression (PHQ-9), and, for DASS-21, 68.5% depression, 76.0% anxiety, 72.4% stress. Sociodemographics alone explained no meaningful variance in depression, anxiety, or stress, but did so for life satisfaction, where emotion features and income were the strongest predictors; neuroticism and network topology instead dominated depression and anxiety, reversing direction between them. Without retraining, RF models separated diaries from low- and high-score personas ($r$ up to 0.91) and, using only network/emotion features, classified clinical from control participants in real transcripts with up to 68% accuracy. These results show the promise and limits of synthetic data: LLM personas can expose model biases, recover patterns consistent with clinical rumination, and support psychometric prediction from human text without a matched questionnaire, but cannot substitute for human validation. NLP Psychometrics makes these distinctions explicit, measurable, and testable through interpretable AI and network/emotional features.

</details>


### [46] [Zero Gap Is Not Restoration: Stratified Per-Question Probability Evaluation and Step-wise Mitigation of Benchmark Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07341)
*Ruijie Hou,Yueyang Jiao,Zhao Wang,Yingming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对测试数据泄漏进预训练语料导致的评估分数虚高问题，指出现有污染缓解评估指标G-AP有缺陷（离散读数无法刻画每题表现、先平均后差分会相互抵消、均匀加权易被策略利用），提出新指标SA-PPG（分层聚合每题概率差）与新缓解方法RailCap（生成过程中基于贪心轨迹兜底判断污染并抑制），实验证明SA-PPG揭露先前策略恢复效果被高估，而RailCap取得最低SA-PPG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共基准测试数据不可避免地泄漏进预训练语料，一旦被记忆就会虚增评估分数。现有污染缓解评估指标G-AP存在根本缺陷：离散的正确/错误读数无法刻画每题难度差异；先平均再差分会让过度抑制与抑制不足相互抵消；均匀每题加权会诱导策略把求解概率推向干净模型的高频值，从而掩盖真实效果。作者希望提出更可靠的评估指标来真实反映缓解策略的效果。

Method: 提出SA-PPG指标：通过采样估计每题求解概率，与干净模型逐题求差，再按干净模型求解概率分层聚合。提出RailCap方法：不像现有策略先估计污染位置再操作（受限于估计准确性），而是生成过程中实时判断——每当样本落回贪心轨迹时，将下一轨迹token封顶为次优选项，累积抑制直到响应分布足够分散。

Result: 在多个受污染模型和基准上，SA-PPG揭示出现有缓解策略的恢复效果被显著高估；RailCap在所有方法中取得最低的SA-PPG，即最有效地恢复了受污染模型的真实能力。

Conclusion: SA-PPG提供了更准确、难以被策略操纵的污染缓解评估框架；RailCap的生成阶段实时抑制策略相比先估计后操作的范式更稳健有效，为后续污染缓解研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Test data from public benchmarks inevitably leaks into pretraining corpora, inflating evaluation scores once memorized. \textbf{Contamination mitigation evaluation} intervenes in the decoding process to suppress memorization and restore a contaminated model's genuine capability, but its prevailing metric, the \textbf{G-AP} (\textbf{G}ap of \textbf{A}ggregate \textbf{P}erformance), is flawed. Discrete correct/incorrect readouts cannot characterize per-question performance, averaging before differencing lets over- and under-suppression cancel out, and uniform per-question weighting invites strategies to push solve probabilities onto the clean model's high-frequency values. We propose \textbf{SA-PPG} (\textbf{S}tratified \textbf{A}ggregate of \textbf{P}er-question \textbf{P}robability \textbf{G}aps): estimate each question's solve probability by sampling, difference it against the clean model per question, and aggregate within groups defined by the clean model's solve probability. Existing mitigation strategies first estimate where contamination lies and then operate on the estimate, so they are only as correct as the estimate. \textbf{RailCap} instead judges contamination during generation: whenever a sample falls back onto the greedy trajectory, the next trajectory token is capped to the runner-up, accumulating suppression until the response distribution becomes sufficiently dispersed. Across multiple contaminated models and benchmarks, SA-PPG reveals that prior strategies' restoration is substantially overestimated, while RailCap attains the lowest SA-PPG.

</details>

*Karim Radouane,Jose G Moreno,Lynda Tamine*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以概念为中心的基准测试（concept-centric benchmark），用于精确探测大语言模型（LLM）对空间概念（方向、距离、拓扑及其组合）的理解能力，评估其抽象性、组合性和接地性（groundness），并通过跨多种模型架构和训练设置的实验揭示了当前LLM在概念理解上的明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作要么使用自然语言基准（容易混淆多种技能），要么使用范围狭窄的合成任务（缺乏对概念及其属性的精确控制），难以真正评估LLM的概念理解能力。作者希望设计一个可控的探测框架，聚焦概念的核心属性来精准衡量LLM的概念获取与组合能力。

Method: 围绕概念的三个核心属性（抽象性、组合性、接地性）设计测试，构建以空间概念（方向、距离、拓扑及其组合）为中心的概念基准，采用问答任务作为代理，在多种LLM架构和训练设置下进行大规模实验，分析模型规模和设计对概念理解的影响。

Result: 实验揭示了当前LLM在概念理解上存在明显局限，并提供了影响其获取和组合结构化概念能力的因素洞见。

Conclusion: 研究发现可为基于概念重新设计LLM提供指导，从而改进信息访问和知识管理；代码已开源。

Abstract: Understanding concepts is fundamental to generalization. Despite their impressive performance on a wide range of tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) still struggle with genuine concept understanding. Prior work has evaluated conceptual understanding in LLMs using natural-language benchmarks or narrowly scoped synthetic tasks, but these settings often conflate multiple skills or lack precise control over the underlying concepts and their properties. To support controlled probing of concepts in LLMs, we design tests on their core properties: abstraction, compositionality, and groundness. We set up a concept-centric benchmark, targeting spatial concepts such as direction, distance, topology, and their compositions, and use question answering tasks serving as a proxy. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple LLM architectures and training regimes to analyze how model scale and design impact conceptual understanding. The results reveal clear limitations in current LLMs and provide insights into the factors shaping their ability to acquire and compose structured concepts. Our findings shed light on how concept-based LLMs can be redesigned for improved information access and knowledge management. The code will be available at https://github.com/rd20karim/concept-probing.

</details>

*Xuye Liu,Yimu Wang,Peng Shi,Bo Xue,Xiangrui Ke,Songcheng Cai,Kath Choi,Di Wu,Freda Shi,Krzysztof Czarnecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LitTraceQA，一个面向科学文献的基准测试集，用于评估文献接地问答系统。系统需要从论文池中检索相关论文、定位具体证据（表格、图表、文本、公式/算法、引用上下文），并以多种格式（自由文本、选择题、结构化表格）生成忠实于证据的答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献被广泛用作语言模型和检索增强生成系统的知识来源，但仅凭流畅的生成能力不足以保证答案可靠。现有系统往往缺乏可验证性，需要能够识别相关论文、定位具体证据并生成忠实于证据答案的可靠系统。为此需要一个专门的基准来评估这类能力。

Method: LitTraceQA要求系统给定研究问题和论文元数据池，输出三个连接的结果：规范化的论文标识符、支持证据的位置、以及一种或多种请求格式的答案（自由文本、选择题、结构化表格）。基准覆盖科学阅读中常见的证据类型：表格、图表、文本片段、公式或算法、引用上下文。开发集包含55个示例（26个隐藏来源单篇论文问题和29个多篇论文问题），并提供金标准论文、证据标注和答案用于本地验证。此外还分析了包含4,978条独特问题记录、覆盖4,859篇独特金标准论文的大规模最终标注集合。

Result: 开发集包含55个示例并提供金标准标注；更大的最终标注集合包含4,978条独特问题记录，覆盖4,859篇独特金标准论文。通过分别评估论文检索、证据接地和答案准确性，LitTraceQA为科学问答系统提供了可验证答案的测试平台。

Conclusion: LitTraceQA作为文献接地科学问答的基准测试集，提供了对论文检索、证据定位和答案生成三个维度的独立评估，旨在推动生成可验证答案而非无依据摘要的科学问答系统发展。

Abstract: Scientific literature is increasingly used as a knowledge source for language models, retrieval-augmented generation systems, and research assistants, but answering research questions from papers requires more than fluent generation. A reliable system must identify the relevant papers, locate the concrete evidence that supports the answer, and produce a response that is faithful to that evidence. We present LitTraceQA, a benchmark for literature-grounded question answering over scientific papers. Given a research question and a metadata pool of papers, a system must return three connected outputs: canonical paper identifiers, supporting evidence locations, and answers in one or more requested formats, including free-form text, multiple-choice answers, and structured tables. LitTraceQA targets evidence types common in scientific reading: tables, figures, text spans, equations or algorithms, and citation contexts. The public development split contains 55 examples, including 26 hidden-source single-paper questions and 29 multi-paper questions, and provides gold papers, evidence annotations, and answers for local validation. We also analyze a larger final annotation collection with 4,978 unique-question records over 4,859 unique gold papers. By evaluating paper retrieval, evidence grounding, and answer accuracy separately, LitTraceQA provides a testbed for scientific QA systems that produce verifiable answers rather than unsupported summaries.

</details>


### [49] [An Exploratory Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Rewriting of Moderate-Complexity Financial Sentences for DisCoCat-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07439)
*Brian Llinas,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在金融情感分析中，利用LLM辅助的受控改写预处理流程，将中等复杂度的句子压缩、简化或分解为更适合DisCoCat量子自然语言处理框架的变体，以解决解析器敏感性和模拟成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的DisCoCat在金融情感分析中存在实际局限性，包括解析器敏感性、高模拟成本以及难以处理较长句子。这限制了QNLP框架在真实金融文本上的可扩展性应用。

Method: 提出LLM辅助的预处理工作流，通过受控改写（压缩、简化、分解）将中等复杂度的金融情感句子转化为解析器兼容、电路高效的形式，同时保留情感含义。对比了不同提示策略、语言模型和过滤配置，并与仅低复杂度输入的DisCoCat基线（Stein等人）进行比较。

Result: 最强的压缩变体在电路层面相比原始中等复杂度子集，平均量子比特数和门数减少超过70%。GPT-4.1-mini配合Prompt B取得最高平均准确率0.550±0.035，优于基线的0.521±0.050。训练集大小与准确率呈中等负相关（Pearson r=-0.446）。

Conclusion: LLM辅助改写可使部分中等复杂度输入在DisCoCat配置中变得可用，为更可扩展的QNLP金融情感分析提供了探索性证据，同时提示设计、过滤和电路感知的预处理是需要重点考虑的因素。

Abstract: Quantum natural language processing (QNLP) provides a grammar-aware framework for text modeling, and Distributional Compositional Categorical (DisCoCat) is one of its theoretically grounded formulations. Prior work on financial sentiment analysis has identified practical limitations of DisCoCat, including parser sensitivity, high simulation cost, and difficulty handling longer sentences. We study an LLM-assisted preprocessing workflow that uses controlled rewriting to compress, simplify, or decompose moderate-complexity financial sentiment sentences into parser-compatible, circuit-efficient variants while preserving sentiment-bearing meaning. We compare prompting strategies, language models, and filtering configurations with the low-complexity-only DisCoCat baseline of Stein et al. At the circuit level, the strongest compression variants reduce average qubit and gate counts by more than 70 percent relative to the raw moderate-complexity subset. Across repeated training runs, GPT-4.1-mini with Prompt B achieves the highest observed mean accuracy, $0.550 \pm 0.035$, compared with $0.521 \pm 0.050$ for the baseline. Larger training splits do not necessarily improve downstream performance; across evaluated configurations, training-split size has a moderately negative association with accuracy (Pearson $r=-0.446$). These results provide exploratory evidence that LLM-assisted rewriting can make some moderate-complexity inputs usable within the evaluated DisCoCat configuration, while highlighting prompt design, filtering, and circuit-aware preprocessing as considerations for more scalable QNLP-based financial sentiment analysis.

</details>


### [50] [CoinRAG: Contextualized Information Nugget KV Cache Reuse for Long-Context RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07458)
*Gyuwan Kim,Cheoneum Park,Tao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoinRAG提出了一种面向长上下文RAG的细粒度KV缓存复用方法，通过两阶段检索识别查询相关的语义单元（"nuggets"），组合复用其KV表示，在保持低预填充延迟的同时提升答案质量，平均F1相对提升5.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG优化工作利用块级KV缓存复用来避免处理冗长的检索上下文，但粗粒度块中仍存在大量信息冗余和噪声，导致准确率受限。需要在低预填充延迟约束下最大化准确率，优化Pareto前沿。

Method: CoinRAG通过两阶段检索在检索到的块内识别与查询相关的语义单元（nuggets），然后组合复用这些离线计算的细粒度KV缓存，并与块级上下文无缝组装，形成紧凑且语义相关的上下文表示，替代全块编码。

Result: 在LongBench多跳问答任务上，CoinRAG显著降低了运营成本，实现了新的Pareto前沿，且在标准快速预填充延迟预算下平均F1相对提升5.3%。

Conclusion: 通过将KV缓存复用粒度从粗粒度的块细化到语义相关的nugget层面，CoinRAG在效率和准确性之间取得了更好的平衡，为低延迟RAG场景提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Recent optimization studies on Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) have exploited chunk-level KV cache reuse to avoid processing long retrieved contexts for higher efficiency, while significant information redundancy and noise still remain in the coarse-grained chunks. This paper optimizes the Pareto frontier under low prefill latency constraints while maximizing accuracy by proposing CoinRAG (Contextualized Information Nugget KV Cache Reuse for Long-Context RAG). The name metaphorically reflects our core mechanism: much like assembling small tokens (or "coins") to accumulate a larger value, CoinRAG compositionally reuses offline-computed, fine-grained nugget caches to form a learned contextual representation efficiently in a more semantically relevant but compact manner. Specifically, instead of full-chunk encoding, CoinRAG identifies query-relevant semantic units within retrieved chunks through two-stage retrieval and seamlessly assembles their sliced KV representations with a chunk-level context. Extensive evaluations on LongBench multi-hop question answering tasks demonstrate that CoinRAG significantly reduces operational costs and outperforms the other baselines with a new Pareto frontier and an average 5.3% relative improvement in answer quality (F1) under a standard fast prefill latency budget.

</details>


### [51] [CreativeInstruct: Scalably Teaching LLMs to Balance Quality, Creativity, and Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07460)
*Ananya Sahu,Mohit Bansal,Elias Stengel-Eskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CreativeInstruct，一种可扩展的指令微调方法，通过在生成中注入[StartCreativity]标记来平衡创造性生成与后训练模型的质量，并引入基于图编辑距离的结构多样性指标。在叙事生成上匹配或超越多模型基线的多样性，且在人类评估中70.3%被认为更具创造性，并作为RL基底带来AMC和MATH指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练（如指令微调、RLHF）虽然提升LLM能力，但降低了输出多样性和创造性，这对显式需要创造力的任务（如故事生成）和隐式需要创造力的任务（如强化学习探索）均有负面影响。

Method: 1) CreativeInstruct：可扩展的指令微调方法，通过训练模型注入[StartCreativity]特殊标记来偏向创造性生成，同时保留后训练模型的质量；2) 提出基于图编辑距离的结构多样性指标，捕捉词汇和语义指标无法捕获的叙事层面变化。

Result: 在叙事生成任务中，CreativeInstruct在多样性上匹配或超过多模型基线和蒸馏变体，且不牺牲质量、推理时无需多模型。人类评估中70.3%认为其生成更具创造性。将GRPO应用于CreativeInstruct检查点，在AMC上提升约4%、MATH上提升约5个百分点，相对于后训练检查点。

Conclusion: 创造性模型不仅本身能产生更高质量的创意文本，还可作为强化学习更好基底，说明在后训练中保留创造力与质量之间的平衡是可行且有益的。

Abstract: While post-training improves the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), it generally lowers their output diversity and creativity, negatively impacting tasks that explicitly require creativity (e.g., story generation) as well as those that require it implicitly, e.g., reinforcement learning (RL). We instead propose CreativeInstruct, a scalable instruction-tuning method that teaches LLMs to balance creative, base-model-like generations with the quality of post-trained models, by learning to inject special [StartCreativity] spans that bias generation toward creativity. Furthermore, we introduce a structural diversity metric based on graph edit distance, which captures narrative level variation missed by purely lexical and semantic metrics. On narrative generation, CreativeInstruct matches or exceeds the diversity of both multi-model baselines and distilled variants of their outputs, without sacrificing quality or requiring multiple models at inference time. These results are mirrored in our human evaluation, where we find that annotators rate CreativeInstruct generations as more creative than the post-trained LLMs' generations in 70.3% of cases. We also show the benefits of creative models as a substrate for RL: GRPO applied to a CreativeInstruct checkpoint improves by ~4% on AMC and ~5% points on MATH over the same training applied to the post-trained checkpoint.

</details>

### [52] [Organizational and Socio-Technical Challenges in UAV Incidents: Evidence from a Practitioner Focus Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06472)
*George Grispos,Joel Elson,Austin Doctor,Suat Cubukcu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过与美国工业界和政府组织的UAV及反UAV从业者进行面对面焦点小组访谈，采用定性分析方法，识别了无人机事件响应中的关键挑战，包括态势感知与空域可见性、碎片化报告与跨组织协调、取证与归因限制、法律政策缺口以及培训与作战能力不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往无人机取证研究多聚焦于从UAV系统中提取技术数据，但缺乏对真实世界UAV事件处理过程及事件响应人员所面临挑战的实证研究。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用面对面焦点小组研究方法，招募来自美国工业界和政府组织的UAV及反UAV从业者，运用定性分析方法对访谈内容进行系统分析，归纳事件响应中的关键挑战。

Result: 识别出五大关键挑战：态势感知与空域可见性不足、报告碎片化与跨组织协调困难、取证与归因局限性、法律政策空白、以及培训和作战能力短板。

Conclusion: 本研究将社会技术事件响应研究拓展至UAV领域，提供了基于从业者视角的无人机事件响应洞察，并为加强该领域的事件响应能力指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles are now widely used across business, government, and recreational contexts, creating new challenges for incident response and digital forensics. While previous forensics research has largely focused on the extraction of technical data from UAV systems, minimal empirical work has examined how UAV incidents are handled in real-world settings or what challenges incident handlers face during this response. To address this gap, this paper reports findings from an in-person focus group with UAV and counter-UAV practitioners from industry and government organizations in the United States. Using qualitative analysis, several key challenges are identified, including situational awareness and airspace visibility, fragmented reporting and interorganizational coordination, forensic and attribution limitations, legal and policy gaps, and shortfalls in training and operational capacity. The research extends socio-technical incident response research to the UAV domain by providing practitioner-driven insight into UAV incident response and highlighting opportunities to strengthen incident response in this domain.

</details>


### [53] [Agentic AI: User Empowerment or Enclosure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06510)
*David Gamba,Daniel M. Romero,Grant Schoenebeck*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过对四个成熟领域（广告拦截器、平台推荐系统、金融机器人顾问、邮件垃圾邮件治理）的比较案例分析，提出"去政治化"（depoliticization）概念在技术系统中的应用：关于代理人（agent）服务谁的利益的决定本质上带有政治性，却被技术安排（API、协议、标准、默认配置）所掩盖。作者认为，个人结果与集体抗争能力可能背道而驰，并将此框架应用于Agentic AI治理，警告其协议（如MCP）和行业组织正在固化配置，使关键选择超出用户和公众的掌控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI承诺赋予用户更大的数字自主权，但其是否能真正赋能用户仍属开放问题。作者认为答案不只是技术问题，而是取决于治理安排中谁的利益被优先服务，因此需要借鉴成熟领域中类似代理形式的经验教训。

Method: 采用比较案例研究法（comparative case analysis），分析四个数字代理较为成熟的领域：浏览器广告拦截器、平台推荐系统、金融机器人顾问和电子邮件垃圾邮件治理，考察其技术安排背后的政治决策。

Result: 研究发现：1）关于代理人服务谁的利益的决定通过技术安排（API选择、协议治理、行业标准、默认配置）被"去政治化"；2）个人结果与集体抗争能力可能反向运动（如垃圾邮件过滤改善但抗争能力崩溃）；3）当中介机构维持对抗性挑战时，用户对齐的代理更持久；当专有基础设施和封闭标准制定吸收抗争时，替代效应加剧。

Conclusion: 作者将分析应用于Agentic AI，指出当前围绕Model Context Protocol和Agentic AI Foundation的治理安排正在固化这些配置，须警惕在决定智能体能力边界的选择超出用户和公众控制之前及时干预。

Abstract: Agentic AI promises a more flexible form of digital agency: systems that can act on users' behalf, from filtering content to negotiating prices to selecting services. Whether it will empower users is an open question, and we argue that the answer depends on more than the technology. We conduct a comparative case analysis of four more mature domains where similar forms of agency arose: browser-based ad blockers, platform recommender systems, financial robo-advisors, and email spam governance. Across the cases, decisions about whose interests agents would serve were resolved through technical arrangements: API choices, protocol governance, industry standards, and default configurations. Beyond their technical form, these were political decisions. We identify this as depoliticization, a concept from political theory, here at work in technological systems. Its most consequential effect is that individual outcomes and collective contestation capacity can move in opposite directions: spam inbox quality improved substantially while the organized capacity to contest spam governance collapsed. Where intermediary institutions sustained adversarial challenge, user-aligned agency proved more durable; where proprietary infrastructure and closed standard-setting absorbed contestation, displacement compounded. We apply this to agentic AI, where governance arrangements consolidating around the Model Context Protocol and the Agentic AI Foundation are settling these configurations before the choices that define what agents can do move outside the reach of users and the public.

</details>


### [54] [Accelerating Accurate Assignment Authoring Using Solution-Generated Autograders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06572)
*Geoffrey Challen,Ben Nordick*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"解决方案生成的自动评测"（solution-generated autograding）方法，通过利用题目作者提供的标准解决方案来自动生成评测器，无需手动枚举测试用例，显著提升评测器的编写速度、准确性和体验。作者实现了Questioner工具（支持Java和Kotlin），并在四年间用于大型CS1课程，编写了近800道编程题，服务数千名学生和数百万次提交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程学习者需要大量练习题目，自动评分器（autograder）是提供快速反馈的关键工具，但手动编写测试用例既繁琐又容易出错，导致评测器不准确，难以快速创建大规模练习题库来支持初学者。

Method: 利用软件测试与自动评分之间的关键差异：题目作者可以提供标准解决方案。从解决方案出发，消除手动枚举测试用例的需求，自动验证评测器准确性，并评估提交代码中除行为正确性以外的其他质量方面。实现了Questioner工具，支持Java和Kotlin语言。

Result: Questioner在大型CS1课程中实际应用四年，作者编写了近800道编程题，服务数千名学生，评估了数百万次代码提交，证明了该方法的可行性和规模化应用效果。

Conclusion: 解决方案生成的自动评分方法能够更快、更准确、更愉快地创建自动评测器，相较于传统手动枚举测试用例的方式具有明显优势，适合大规模编程教育场景。

Abstract: Students learning to program benefit from access to large numbers of practice problems. Autograders are commonly used to support programming questions by providing quick feedback on submissions. But authoring accurate autograders remains challenging. Autograders are frequently created by enumerating test cases--a tedious process that can produce inaccurate autograders that fail to correctly classify submissions. When authoring accurate autograders is slow, it is difficult to create large banks of practice problems to support beginning programmers.
  We present solution-generated autograding: a faster, more accurate, and more enjoyable way to create autograders. Our approach leverages a key difference between software testing and autograding: The question author can provide a solution. By starting with a solution, we can eliminate the need to manually enumerate test cases, validate the autograder's accuracy, and evaluate other aspects of submission code quality beyond behavioral correctness. We describe Questioner, an implementation of solution-generated autograding for Java and Kotlin, and share experiences from four years using Questioner to support a large CS1 course: authoring nearly 800 programming questions used by thousands of students to evaluate millions of submissions.

</details>


### [55] [Implementation of Split Deadlines in a Large CS1 Course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06753)
*Hongxuan Chen,Ang Li,Geoffrey Challen,Kathryn Cunningham*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文报告了一项在大型CS1课程中实施的错峰截止日期（split deadlines）政策研究。将学生随机分为两组，错开作业发布和截止日期，每组获得相同完成时间但每期使用办公时间的学生数量减半。研究发现该政策提高了办公时间使用效率，两组学生成绩无显著差异，且大多数学生认为该政策公平有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学课程中办公时间的使用在截止日期临近时会出现高峰，导致学生等待时间长、教职工工作过载的问题。作者希望通过错峰截止日期政策来缓解高峰期的办公时间压力。

Method: 在一门大型CS1课程中实施错峰截止日期政策：将学生随机分为两组，错开作业的发布与截止日期，每组拥有相同的作业完成时间，但同一天截止的学生数量减少一半。通过测量办公时间使用率、教职工效率、学生成绩表现及学生对该政策公平性和有效性的感知来评估政策效果。

Result: 错峰截止日期政策提高了办公时间的效率；两组学生之间的成绩没有显著差异；大多数学生认为该政策公平且有效。

Conclusion: 错峰截止日期政策是一种可行且有效的方案，既能缓解办公时间高峰压力，又不损害学生学业表现。论文还提供了实施和改进类似政策的经验反馈与建议。

Abstract: Office hour utilization in computer science courses can spike near deadlines, producing long wait times, frustrated students, and overworked staff. To address this problem, a large CS1 course implemented a split deadlines policy. Students were randomly divided into two groups with staggered release and due dates. Each group had the same amount of time to complete assignments, but the number of students with each due date was reduced by half.
  Our study evaluates the effectiveness of this policy. We measure office hour utilization and staff efficiency near deadlines, examine the policy's impact on student performance, and investigate student perception of the policy's fairness and effectiveness. Overall we found that the split deadline policy increased office hour efficiency, resulted in no significant difference in performance between groups, and was considered fair and effective by most students. Our experience report includes reflections and student feedback indicating how to implement and further improve similar policies.

</details>


### [56] [Investigating the Presence and Development of Student Instructor Preferences in a Large-Scale CS1 Course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06782)
*Yiqiu Zhou,Luc Paquette,Geoffrey Challen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究利用一个新型学习平台的数据，追踪了整个学期中学生对多位教师教学内容偏好的形成与演变过程，发现偏好是动态变化的，且学生特征（性别、编程经验）与最终偏好存在显著关联，但与初始偏好无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究已确认学生-教师偏好的重要性并识别了多种影响因素，但由于常见课程结构的局限和一次性测量的依赖，学生对教师偏好如何形成和变化的过程尚不明确。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用一个新型学习平台的数据，该平台允许学生访问多位教师创建的教学内容。通过追踪学生在整个学期中反复选择不同教师内容的行为，量化偏好的出现与演变，并比较初始偏好与最终偏好，同时分析与学生特征（性别、编程经验等）的关联。

Result: 初始偏好与最终偏好的比较表明偏好是一个被经历持续塑造的动态构建。学生属性与初始偏好无显著相关，但在最终偏好上，不同性别和自报编程经验的学生之间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 该研究拓展了对计算机科学教育中师生关系与学生偏好的理解，并为多教师协作授课的机构和教师提供了实践启示。

Abstract: Prior research has established the importance of student instructor preferences and identified various influencing factors. However, the dynamics of how student instructor preferences develop and change are less well understood, due to the limitations of common course structures and reliance on one-time measurements. To bridge this gap, we utilize data from a novel learning platform that provides students with access to instructional content created by multiple instructors. This platform enables the quantification of preference emergence and evolution throughout an entire semester, as students repeatedly select content from different instructors. Examining both initial and final student instructor preferences suggests that preference is a dynamic construct continually shaped by experiences. Furthermore, our analysis of the associations between preferences and student characteristics reveals a nuanced picture: while student attributes did not significantly correlate with initial preferences, substantial differences emerged in final preferences across genders and self-reported prior programming experience. This analysis contributes to the existing body of knowledge by expanding our understanding of student instructor preferences and student-instructor relationships in computer science education. We also provide practical insights that institutions and instructors can draw on when multiple instructors collaborate on a course.

</details>


### [57] ["Death by a thousand taxonomies?": AI Risk Classification In Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06831)
*Glen Berman,Ned Cooper,Angel Hsing-Chi Hwang,Wesley Hanwen Deng,Renee Shelby,Ben Hutchinson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过对25位跨行业、学术界、公民社会和政府的专家访谈，实证研究了社会技术结果分类法(SOT)在AI治理中的开发与使用情况，发现SOT与AI治理流程整合薄弱，原因在于其设计选择对下游用户不可见，且列举危害时未与决策点和行动者关联，导致问责困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI带来的危害范围从用户交互到社会层面广泛且多样，对AI风险进行分类是治理的基础，监管者、科技公司和政策制定者需要结构化的风险描述作为行动依据。然而SOT的实际使用效果缺乏实证研究。

Method: 基于25次半结构化访谈，受访者来自产业界、学术界、公民社会和政府部门，对SOT的开发和使用进行质性实证研究。

Result: 发现SOT在AI治理流程中整合薄弱。识别出两个关键原因：一是SOT设计选择对下游用户不可见，用户将分类视为风险的详尽清单而非解释性辅助工具；二是SOT通常只枚举危害，未将其与决策点或相关行动者关联，使问责难以落实。

Conclusion: 为SOT开发者和使用者提出设计建议，并指出发挥SOT潜力需要尚不存在的治理基础设施。

Abstract: The harms in which AI is implicated range in nature and scope from unsafe user interactions through to the societal-wide consequences of AI adoption. Classification of the diverse risks of AI is foundational to AI governance: regulators, technology firms, and policymakers need structured accounts of risk upon which to act. Researchers and practitioners have accordingly developed many Sociotechnical Outcome Taxonomies (SOT). This paper presents an empirical study of SOT development and use, drawing on 25 interviews with researchers and practitioners across industry, academia, civil society, and government. We find SOT are weakly integrated into AI governance processes, and identify two features of SOT design and use that explain why. First, the design choices through which SOT produce structured representations of the complex problem space of AI risks tend to be invisible to downstream taxonomy users. Those users treat the resulting categories as exhaustive accounts of risk rather than as interpretive aids. Second, SOT typically enumerate harms without linking them to decision points or actors implicated in their occurrence, leaving accountability difficult to assign. We close with design recommendations for SOT developers and users, and argue realising the potential of SOT requires governance infrastructure that does not yet exist.

</details>


### [58] [Where Does AI Innovation Go? Measuring Research Attention Imbalance in AI Music](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06903)
*Qian Liang,Yanzhen Ning,Fengyuan Zhang,Bo Dai,Ningbo Cheng*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文分析了2015至2026年4月的6,839篇AI音乐论文，提出"研究注意力轮廓"框架，包含技术投入、方法分配、方法多样性和前沿方法采用滞后四个指标，发现研究注意力集中在可扩展的内容导向任务上，而教育、健康、治理等领域支持不足，且各领域在前沿方法采用速度上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI音乐研究虽快速增长，但缺乏系统性框架来衡量不同音乐任务之间研究注意力的不均衡分配。现有研究从技术、应用或文献计量等单独视角出发，无法全面测量领域层面的不平衡。

Method: 构建了12个应用类别与11个技术方法家族的联合分类体系，分析6,839篇AI音乐论文（2015至2026年4月），提出包含四个指标（技术投入、方法分配、方法多样性、前沿方法采用滞后）的研究注意力轮廓（Research Attention Profile）。

Result: 技术支撑集中在可扩展、内容导向的任务上；教育、健康和治理领域支持不足。生成任务平均仅0.33年即采用前沿方法，而教育领域需要4.33年，健康领域需要5.00年，揭示了方法发展的不均衡性。

Conclusion: 研究注意力在不同音乐任务间存在显著不均衡，这种不均衡阻碍了教育、健康等社会价值领域的AI音乐发展，需要更对社会负责的AI音乐研究议程。

Abstract: The rapid growth of artificial intelligence (AI) in music has expanded research from generation and information retrieval to education, health, and governance. Yet this growth does not necessarily imply balanced research attention. Where is research attention directed across diverse music tasks, and how can such imbalance be systematically measured? Existing studies examine AI music from separate technical, application-specific, or bibliometric perspectives, but lack a systematic framework for measuring field-level imbalance. To address this gap, we analyze 6,839 AI music publications from 2015 to April 2026 using a joint taxonomy of 12 application categories and 11 technical method families. We propose the Research Attention Profile, comprising four indicators of technical investment, method allocation, methodological diversity, and frontier-method adoption lag. Results show that technical support is concentrated in scalable, content-oriented tasks, while education, health, and governance remain under-supported. Generation adopts frontier methods after only 0.33 years on average, compared with 4.33 years for education and 5.00 years for health. These findings reveal uneven methodological development and support a more socially responsive AI music research agenda.

</details>


### [59] [Educational Short Videos: Bibliometric Trends, Thematic Structure, and Operationalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06932)
*Yidi Zhang,Jindi Wang,Pedro Bem-Haja,Zhipeng Li,Lei Shi,Thomas K. F. Chiu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过文献计量学、NMF主题建模和结构化内容分析，对截至2026年6月11日Web of Science和Scopus中2169条教育短视频研究记录进行了系统映射，揭示了该领域的主题组织、发展轨迹和操作化特征，并提出了基于功能而非时长定义教育短视频的工作定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育短视频研究跨越多个学科、平台和学习情境，但同一标签被应用于功能、活动、情境和评价各异的资源，导致研究比较困难且证据综合受阻，亟需对该领域的发展脉络和概念边界进行系统梳理。

Method: 综合运用三种方法：1）文献计量分析（2,169条WoS和Scopus记录）；2）非负矩阵分解（NMF）主题建模（识别16个一级主题）；3）结构化内容分析（考察操作化方式、结果领域和研究设计）。

Result: 主要发现包括：出版量自2010年代中期急剧增长但分布分散；技能发展主题构成领域结构核心；健康干预和认知负荷两类主题兼具增长与高引文可见度；社交媒体参与策略为快速成长方向；知识/成就和参与/动机是最常见结果领域；实验和综合设计在健康相关主题中更常见；索引描述最常强调目标用户、教育用途、学习内容和交互性；代表性时长可获得于511条记录，但未发现一致的数值阈值。

Conclusion: 提出教育短视频的工作定义——"结合文字和视觉的离散多媒体信息，设计或用于促进学习"，并强调"短"应相对于教育目的、内容单元和周围活动来解释，而非仅依据时长。这为跨学科比较和未来证据综合提供了概念基础。

Abstract: Educational short-video research spans disciplines, platforms and learning contexts, but the same label is applied to resources differing in function, activity, context and evaluation, complicating comparison and evidence synthesis. This study mapped the development, thematic organisation and operationalisation of educational short-video research. We analysed 2,169 records indexed in Web of Science and Scopus up to 11 June 2026 using bibliometric analysis, non-negative matrix factorisation topic modelling and structured content analysis. Publication output increased sharply from the mid-2010s but remained dispersed across outlets. Among 16 first-level topics, Skill Development in Educational Contexts was the largest, forming the structural core of the field, while topic overlap was predominantly pairwise. Video-Based Health Interventions for Attitude Change and Cognitive Load and Engagement in Instructional Video Design combined positive recent growth with comparatively high citation visibility, whereas Social Media Engagement Strategies for Education emerged as a rapidly growing direction. Knowledge/Achievement and Engagement/Motivation were the most widely represented outcome domains, and experimental and synthesis designs were more common among identifiable records in health-related topics. Indexed descriptions most often foregrounded intended users, educational uses, learning content, and interactivity. A representative duration was available for 511 records, but no consistently applied numerical threshold was evident. These findings support a working definition of educational short videos as discrete multimedia messages combining words and visuals and designed or used to promote learning. Shortness should be interpreted relative to educational purpose, content unit and surrounding activity rather than duration alone.

</details>


### [60] [Data Annotation as Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07297)
*Emma Harvey,Allison Koenecke,Rene F. Kizilcec*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文主张将数据标注视为一种测量问题，而非仅仅追求标注者间的一致性。作者通过文献综述（N=132）和访谈（N=10），提出了一个诊断和修正标注问题的框架，包括标注流程中的关键决策点、五类标注问题来源（错误、歧义、不可能性、主观性、标注者身份），以及超越一致性评估信度和效度的实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统依赖标注数据，但标注质量常被简化为"一致性"指标，即多个标注者同则视为高质量。然而一致性并不能证明标注有效捕捉了其旨在表征的底层概念。作者认为标注应被视为一种测量行为，需要系统的概念定义、工具操作化、应用和评估框架。

Method: 采用混合研究方法：(1)对132篇标注质量研究进行文献综述；(2)对10名标注团队成员进行半结构化访谈。基于这两部分数据，开发了一个诊断和修正标注问题的框架，涵盖标注流程的关键决策点、问题来源分类，以及测量理论向实践指南的转化。

Result: 研究产出三项主要成果：(1)映射了标注流程中的关键决策点（任务设计、标注者管理、质量评估、质量改进、裁决）；(2)识别了五类标注问题来源：错误、歧义、不可能性、主观性和标注者身份，并指出结果层面相似的问题常需基于来源采取不同的过程级干预；(3)将测量理论转化为超越一致性指标的可靠性和有效性评估实践指南。

Conclusion: 通过将标注重新框定为测量问题，本文为提升AI研究和实践中标注数据质量提供了概念基础，强调标注团队应超越简单的一致性度量，从测量理论视角系统性地评估和改进标注过程。

Abstract: Modern AI systems depend on annotated data, but annotation is rarely treated as the act of measurement that it is. Instead, annotation quality is commonly reduced to agreement: if multiple annotators assign the same annotation to a data instance, the annotations are taken to be high-quality. Yet agreement does not establish whether annotations validly capture the underlying concept they are meant to represent. In this paper, we argue that data annotation should be understood as a measurement problem. Like other forms of measurement, annotation requires defining a concept, operationalizing it through an instrument, applying that instrument, and evaluating the reliability and validity of the resulting measurements. Drawing on a literature review of annotation quality research (N=132) and semi-structured interviews with annotation team members (N=10), we develop a framework for diagnosing and correcting annotation issues. First, we map key decision points across annotation processes - including task design, annotator management, quality assessment, quality improvement, and adjudication - that shape annotation outcomes. Second, we identify five distinct sources of annotation issues: error, ambiguity, impossibility, subjectivity, and annotator identity. Annotation problems that appear similar at the level of outcomes often require different process-level interventions based on their sources. Finally, we translate measurement theory into practical guidance for annotation teams, showing how assessments of reliability and validity can move beyond agreement alone. By reframing annotation as measurement, we offer a conceptual foundation for improving the quality of annotated data used in AI research and practice.

</details>


### [61] [From Forensics to Ecosystems: Rethinking Watermarks for Generative AI Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07337)
*Daniel Susser,John Thickstun,Gili Vidan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了使用数字水印进行AI治理的挑战与机遇，特别是在AI生成文本领域。作者批评现有观点将水印视为孤立的取证工具，提出重新概念化数字水印为理解合成内容对媒体生态系统影响的工具，而非可靠识别单个合成内容的手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的普及引发了深刻的认知与社会焦虑，以及难以解决的治理问题。数字水印作为一种看似可行的治理路径引起了监管者的兴趣和研究者的怀疑，但批评者认为其在技术上脆弱、认知上模糊、政治上无效。作者希望回应这些批评并提供一个更有效的概念框架。

Method: 作者通过概念性论证和框架重构，提出"生态系统方法"（ecosystems approach）来重新理解数字水印在AI治理中的作用，将其从个体层面的数字取证工具转变为系统层面的媒体生态影响评估工具。

Result: 作者论证了生态系统方法能更有效地利用水印的特性，并且虽然这种方法会带来自身的治理挑战，但这些挑战比将水印用于数字取证的挑战更易于解决。

Conclusion: 数字水印不应被视为可靠识别单个AI生成内容的取证工具，而应被重新定位为理解合成内容对媒体生态系统影响的治理工具，这种重构使水印治理变得更加可行。

Abstract: The arrival of generative AI as a cheap, widely accessible commercial service, and the tidal wave of AI-generated synthetic content it has unleashed, have provoked deep epistemic and social anxieties and raised difficult governance questions that policymakers are struggling to address. One approach that has attracted both enthusiasm from regulators and skepticism from researchers is digital watermarking. Signals embedded in a synthetically-generated piece of content indicating that it was AI-generated---possibly even identifying the specific systems that generated it---appear to offer a path toward mitigating risks of genAI that avoids the downsides of more interventionist strategies. But critics warn that watermarks may prove technically brittle, epistemically ambiguous, and politically ineffectual tools. In this paper, we explore the challenges and opportunities of using digital watermarking for AI governance, paying special attention to the specific problem of watermarking AI-generated text. We argue that such critiques often treat the problem of identifying synthetic content as an isolated forensic question. Instead, we propose reconceptualizing digital watermarks as tools for understanding the impacts of synthetic content on media ecosystems, rather than reliably identifying individual pieces of synthetic content. Such an ``ecosystems approach'' more effectively utilizes the features of watermarks. And while this approach raises its own governance challenges, we argue that they are more tractable than the challenges of using watermarks for digital forensics.

</details>


### [62] [Against Explainable Artificial Intelligence In Law: Why Justifiable Ai Matters. A Credit Scoring Example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07452)
*Łukasz Górski*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能在信用评分中的应用所引发的可解释性问题。作者回顾了欧盟相关法律背景，并结合技术科学的见解来解释法律条款，拒绝了狭隘的"解释权"解读，主张应采用广泛的解释视角——不仅包括技术性解释，还包括法律理由说明，以有效保障债权人的权利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI和机器学习在信用评分等金融应用中的日益普及，模型复杂度的提升引发了对可解释性等问题的担忧。法律对"解释权"的理解存在歧义，需要结合技术现实进行法律解读，以在保护各方权益的同时不阻碍技术发展。

Method: 本文采用跨学科研究方法：一方面回顾和梳理欧盟相关的法律背景（如GDPR等法规），另一方面整合技术科学领域关于可解释AI（XAI）的见解，在此基础上对相关法律条款进行技术可行性视角下的重新诠释。

Result: 作者论证了狭义的"解释权"解读（仅指技术性解释）的不充分性，并提出广泛的解释应包括技术解释和法律理由说明两个层面，这种广泛的解读是唯一能够以可操作的方式保障债权人权利的方式。

Conclusion: 采用广泛的解释权解读是必要的，它将技术性解释与法律正当性论证相结合，从而在AI信用评分的实际应用中既能维护监管合规，又能有效保护债权人的合法权利。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence-based solutions offer new efficiency-increasing possibilities in many applications, including credit scoring. Yet, the increasing sophistication of machine-learning models in use raises concerns regarding many of their aspects, explainability notwithstanding. We review the relevant EU legal background and integrate this review with insights from technical sciences to interpret relevant legal provisions in the light of technological possibilities. We reject the narrow interpretations of the right to explanation and suggest the broad one, which encompasses not only a technical explanations but also a legal justification as the only one that allows to safeguard the creditors rights in an operative manner.

</details>
