Motivation: 现有VLN系统将预训练VLM适配为VLA策略，直接将观测和指令映射到动作，但这种以动作中心的训练没有显式建模观测在预测运动下应如何演化。虽然已有生成式世界动作模型（WAM）可联合预测未来观测和动作，但现有连续VLN的WAM未将联合未来视角与动作生成条件化于从观测历史推断的几何感知表示上。

Method: 1) 冻结的前馈几何编码器从单目RGB历史提取几何感知表示；2) 可训练的3D Scene-to-Token适配器将几何表示转换为世界动作Diffusion Transformer token空间中的固定长度前缀；3) 通过块级因果注意力，前缀条件化每个未来视频-动作块，提供共享几何上下文；4) 训练分三阶段：A*演示上的监督世界动作微调、策略访问状态的DAgger式适配、基于DanceGRPO的闭环策略优化。

Result: 在GN-Bench上，WNM-3D在闭环导航中超越了强VLM导航策略及其2D条件版本。在固定近目标评估集上，WNM-3D实现了更高的流-动作一致性和更低的视觉运动误差。

Conclusion: 在连续VLN中引入3D场景几何条件化到生成式世界-动作模型中，能有效提升闭环导航性能，并同时改善动作一致性和视觉运动预测精度，验证了几何感知世界建模对VLA策略的重要性。

Abstract: Recent vision-language navigation (VLN) systems increasingly adapt pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into vision-language-action (VLA) policies that map egocentric observations and language instructions directly to navigation actions. Although semantically capable, such action-centric training does not explicitly model how the agent's visual observations should evolve under its predicted motion. Generative world-action models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and actions, yet existing WAMs for continuous VLN do not condition joint future-view and action generation on geometry-aware representations inferred from the observed history. We present WNM-3D, a generative World Navigation Model with 3D scene conditioning for continuous VLN. To consolidate past observations into persistent scene context, a frozen feed-forward geometry encoder extracts geometry-aware representations from the monocular egocentric RGB history, and a trainable 3D Scene-to-Token Adapter converts them into a fixed-length prefix in the token space of the world-action Diffusion Transformer. Through block-causal attention, this prefix conditions every future video-action block, providing a shared geometric context for both future-view and action generation. We train WNM-3D through supervised world-action fine-tuning on A*-generated demonstrations, DAgger-style adaptation on policy-visited states, and DanceGRPO-based closed-loop policy optimization. Experiments on GN-Bench show that WNM-3D outperforms strong VLM-based navigation policies and its 2D-conditioned counterpart in closed-loop navigation. On a fixed near-goal evaluation set, WNM-3D also achieves higher flow-action consistency and lower visual-motion error.

</details>


### [231] [Winning by Peeking: Unenforced Budgets and Test-Set Selection Inflate Short-Budget AutoML Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07303)
*Guilin Zhang,Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Orcetra引擎的案例研究，揭示了短时间预算下AutoML系统比较中的协议缺陷：测试集泄漏（在测试集上选择最佳候选）和预算执行不严导致虚假的胜率优势；修正协议后Orcetra的胜率从59.4%降至34.3%，不再显著优于基线。作者还测量了选择偏差随预算的变化，并提出短预算比较的检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短时间预算（数十秒而非数小时）下的AutoML系统比较在工具README和研讨会论文中很常见，但容易出错。作者想通过一个实际案例展示这些比较中的协议缺陷如何产生误导性结论，并提供改进的比较方法。

Method: 作者进行了案例研究：Orcetra引擎在513个OpenML数据集上与FLAML和AutoGluon竞争。通过记录单一搜索内的两个估计量（选择规则和计算资源），分解胜率下降的归因（选择规则占4.8个百分点，不等计算占大部分），并测量选择偏差作为预算的函数。

Result: 修正协议后，Orcetra的胜率从59.4%降至34.3%，与任一竞争者的成对差异不再显著。选择偏差随K增长但仅达0.27精度点，约为边际标准误差论证预测的σ√(2lnK)界的五分之一，因为共享测试行的候选评分抵消了大部分噪声。

Conclusion: 短预算AutoML比较的误导性结果主要来自协议缺陷（测试集泄漏和不严格的时间预算），而非算法本身的优劣；作者提供了短预算比较的检查清单，并发布代码和可复现的脚本。

Abstract: Comparisons between AutoML systems at short time budgets -- tens of seconds rather than hours -- are common in tool READMEs and workshop papers, and they are easy to get wrong. We report a case study in which a simple AutoML engine, Orcetra, appeared to beat FLAML and AutoGluon on 513 OpenML datasets, winning 57.1% of them at a nominal 60-second budget and 78.4% of datasets against FLAML alone at 30 seconds. Both margins came from protocol defects that a results table cannot show. The search loop scored every candidate on the test split and reported the best, making the headline metric a maximum over dozens of noisy estimates while the baselines selected on training data and touched the test set once; and the budget was checked before launching a candidate but never enforced during one, so the system consumed a median of 120 s against a 60-second budget, 2.24x the wall-clock AutoGluon used. Re-running with selection moved to a validation split, the deadline enforced externally and every framework pinned to an equal share of the machine, Orcetra's win rate on the re-run subset falls from 59.4% to 34.3% and no pairwise difference against either competitor remains significant. Recording both estimands inside a single search lets us attribute the collapse: the selection rule accounts for 4.8 percentage points and unequal compute for most of the rest. The same traces give the selection bias as a function of budget, measured rather than assumed: it grows with $K$ but reaches only 0.27 accuracy points, about five times below the $σ\sqrt{2\ln K}$ bound a marginal-standard-error argument predicts, because candidates scored on shared test rows cancel most of the noise. We close with a checklist for short-budget comparisons. Code, per-dataset results and the scripts that regenerate every number and figure in the paper are released with it.

</details>


### [232] [An End-to-End Agent Auditing Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07346)
*Haoning Wang,Mingxun Zhang,Chenyue Yu,Yingjun Shang,Xia Hu,Guanchu Wang,Na Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了A²E（Agent Auditing Engine），一个端到端的智能体框架（harness）评估引擎。通过Agent Task Protocol（ATP）实现任务快速集成，利用自动插桩的Monitor捕获标准化执行轨迹，并在评估阶段使用多维指标（执行效率、工具使用、任务规划、错误恢复）对框架能力进行系统评估。实验表明模型-框架组合在不同任务上表现差异显著，没有单一组合能在所有任务上持续最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM的快速发展，智能体框架（harness）已成为部署智能体的关键基础设施，但如何高效构建端到端、系统化、全面的评估流水线仍是重大挑战。

Method: 1) 提出Agent Task Protocol (ATP)协议，实现评估任务与不同harness的快速集成；2) 通过自动插桩的Monitor捕获并生成标准化执行轨迹；3) 在评估阶段使用多维指标体系，涵盖执行效率、工具使用、任务规划和错误恢复等方面，而非仅依赖正确率。

Result: 实验发现模型-框架组合在不同类型任务上存在显著的性能差异，没有任何单一组合能在所有任务上持续优于其他组合。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了系统化评估的必要性，并为模型与harness的协同演进提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), harnesses have become essential infrastructure for deploying agents across a wide range of domains. The fast-evolving harness ecosystem has also made rigorous capability evaluation increasingly important. However, efficiently building an end-to-end, systematic, and comprehensive evaluation pipeline remains a significant challenge. To address this challenge, we introduce $A^2E$ (Agent Auditing Engine), an end-to-end evaluation engine designed for agent harnesses. $A^2E$ leverages our newly proposed Agent Task Protocol (ATP) to enable the rapid integration of evaluation tasks with different harnesses. Through an automatically instrumented Monitor, it captures and generates standardized execution traces during experiments. In the Evaluation stage, $A^2E$ systematically assesses harness capabilities using a suite of multidimensional metrics. Compared with correctness alone, these metrics provide a more fine-grained characterization of differences among harnesses in execution efficiency, tool use, task planning, and error recovery. Experiments conducted with $A^2E$ further reveal that model-harness combinations exhibit substantial performance variation across different types of tasks, and that no single combination consistently outperforms all others across every task. These findings not only demonstrate the necessity of systematic evaluation but also provide useful guidance for the co-evolving of models and harnesses. Our code is available at https://github.com/datamllab/A2E.

</details>


### [233] [QFCQT: A Chaotically Gated Quantformer Framework for Volatile Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07363)
*Junkai Lin,Siqi Hou,Raymond Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QFCQT（量子分形启发的混沌门控Quantformer），用于非平稳时间序列预测，结合Quantformer编码器、Lee振荡器激活模块和光滑混沌门控融合机制，在ETTh1、ETTh2和A股指数基准上优于Informer、LogTrans、LSTMa、HAT和COTN等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳时间序列预测困难，因其存在长程依赖、局部波动爆发、结构性突变和非线性振荡行为。尽管Transformer类预测器擅长建模长期时序依赖，其前馈块多依赖平滑静态激活函数，对突然的区间转换不够敏感。

Method: QFCQT由三个主要组件构成：(1) Quantformer风格数值编码器，通过线性嵌入直接处理多变量输入；(2) 可学习的Lee振荡器激活模块，将标量预激活映射为动态振荡响应，并通过Max-over-Time池化汇总；(3) 光滑混沌门控融合机制，自适应平衡常规平滑激活与混沌敏感响应。此外，采用八个参数化Lee振荡器族的软叠加（soft superposition）来适应不同区间的非线性响应模式。

Result: 在ETTh1、ETTh2和A股股票指数三个基准上，QFCQT一致性地优于包括Informer、LogTrans、LSTMa、HAT和COTN在内的强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入量子分形启发的振荡器激活与混沌门控机制，QFCQT有效提升了Transformer在复杂易变动态下的非平稳时间序列预测鲁棒性，验证了自适应非线性激活对捕捉区间转换的价值。

Abstract: Forecasting non-stationary time series remains difficult due to long-range dependencies, local volatility bursts, structural shifts, and nonlinear oscillatory behaviors. Although Transformer-based forecasters are effective for modeling long-term temporal dependencies, their feed-forward blocks typically rely on smooth static activations that are insufficiently sensitive to abrupt regime changes. Motivated by quantitative Transformer designs and oscillator-based nonlinear activations, we propose QFCQT, short for Quantum-Fractal-inspired Chaotically Gated Quantformer, for robust forecasting under complex volatile dynamics. Here, "quantum-fractal-inspired" denotes a computational analogy based on soft oscillator superposition and multi-scale nonlinear responses, rather than a formal quantum-mechanical or fractal-theoretic derivation. QFCQT consists of three main components: (1) a Quantformer-style numerical encoder that directly processes multivariate inputs via linear embedding; (2) a learnable Lee-oscillator activation module that maps scalar pre-activations to dynamic oscillatory responses and summarizes them through Max-over-Time pooling; and (3) a smooth-chaotic gated fusion mechanism that adaptively balances conventional smooth activations and chaos-sensitive responses. Furthermore, instead of using a single fixed oscillator, QFCQT employs a soft superposition of eight parameterized Lee oscillator families to adaptively capture different nonlinear response patterns across regimes. Experiments on ETTh1, ETTh2, and A-share Stock Index benchmarks show that QFCQT consistently outperforms strong baselines, including Informer, LogTrans, LSTMa, HAT, and COTN.

</details>


### [234] [People Are Not Just Their Countries. Disentangling Social Determinants of LLM Value Alignment Across Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07367)
*Maria-Louisa Wightman,Guillaume Bied,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用欧洲社会调查数据，考察了10个主流商业大语言模型（LLM）在价值观上与不同社会人口群体的对齐程度。研究发现LLM确实与不同社会人口群体（尤其是教育、收入、职业和宗教定义的群体）的价值观存在不对齐现象；在国家层面，受访者所在国家单独就能解释与全部社会人口变量相当程度的变异，且国家因素与社会人口因素在对齐模式解释上是互补的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM成为主要信息来源和咨询对象，理解它们与人类价值观的对齐变得迫切。已有研究大多以国家边界或文化界限定义研究人群，忽视了社会人口分界线（如教育、收入、职业、宗教等）在价值观对齐差异中可能发挥的作用。

Method: 利用欧洲社会调查（European Social Survey）数据，针对10个主流商业LLM，从15个社会人口变量以及居住国角度，分析LLM与不同群体人类价值观的对齐程度。研究既从群体层面考察不同社会人口变量的对齐差异，也从个体层面比较国家变量与全部社会人口变量的解释力，并进一步区分国家层面与社会人口因素在解释价值对齐模式中的相对作用。

Result: LLM对不同社会人口群体的价值观存在不对齐现象，尤其在教育、收入、职业和宗教等维度上表现突出。在个体层面，受访者所在国家单独就能解释相当大程度的变异，其解释力与全部社会人口变量的总和相当。国家因素与社会人口因素在对齐模式解释上是互补关系，二者的相对权重会随所考察的问题子集不同而变化。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了LLM价值对齐研究中忽视社会人口分界线的知识空白，表明价值对齐偏差不仅存在于国家/文化层面，也存在于社会人口群体层面。研究结果强调了在评估和改进LLM价值对齐时，需要同时考虑国家和社会人口两个维度的因素。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as a primary source of information and advice, understanding their alignment to humans in terms of values becomes a pressing concern. A growing literature has leveraged large scale surveys to investigate to what extent LLMs' and humans' stated values and opinions align. With limited exceptions, studied populations have been defined country borders or cultural bounds. Yet, this focus neglects the role that socio-demographic divides may play for value alignment disparities.
  Relying on the European Social Survey, we address this knowledge gap by considering value alignment displayed with respect to 10 prominent commercial LLMs in terms of 15 socio-demographic variables as well as country of residence. Our analyses reveal that LLMs are indeed unequally aligned to the values of different socio-demographic groups, notably those defined by education, income, occupation and religion. When examining alignment at the individual level, a respondent's country, taken as a stand-alone variable, explains a substantial amount of variation that is on par with the full set of considered socio-demographics. Further disentangling the respective role of country-level and socio-demographic factors, we find they are complementary in explaining value alignment patterns, with their relative weights varying across the subset of questions considered.

</details>


### [235] [CoBa: Cost-Effective Test-Time Scaling via Compute-Balanced Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07424)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoBa计算平衡路由策略，将测试时推理形式化为计算分配问题，通过先少量生成、广泛廉价验证、再对不确定候选做强验证的方式，在固定推理预算下实现更优的准确率-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试时扩展（test-time scaling）通常沿单一维度投入计算（采样更多解、延长思维链或提供更强的评估器），在固定推理预算下这些选择相互竞争，缺乏对计算分配的系统性优化。作者希望将测试时推理建模为计算分配问题，回答"下一个计算单元应花在生成、验证还是停止上"这一核心问题。

Method: 提出CoBa（Compute-Balanced Routing）策略，包含三个阶段：1）先生成一小批候选解；2）对全部候选进行廉价验证（broad cheap verification）；3）将不确定或高价值的候选路由到更强验证器。通过这种分层验证与路由机制，实现计算资源的平衡分配。

Result: 在3,129个示例-生成器评估（涵盖MATH-500、AIME 2024/2025、AMC 2023及程序化符号推理）上，CoBa-Routed-Strong达到85.13%宏平均准确率，与自评估加权投票代理（85.20%）统计匹配，但节省49.1%的参数加权token；与最佳16选多数投票相比，在58.9%更少token下仅差0.01个宏平均准确率点。配对bootstrap检验显示相对单样本解码有显著提升。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展的关键在于"下一步计算投放到哪里最具价值"，而非单纯增加某一维度的计算量。CoBa证明了计算平衡路由策略可以显著降低计算成本同时保持性能，但离池化算子（pool oracle）仍有差距，说明更精准的路由策略仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Test-time scaling is often implemented by spending more compute along one axis: sampling more solutions, extending a chain of thought, or applying a stronger evaluator. Under a fixed inference budget, these choices compete. This paper formulates test-time reasoning as a compute-allocation problem in which a system must decide whether the next unit of compute should be spent on generation, verification, or stopping. We introduce CoBa, a compute-balanced routing policy that first obtains a small set of candidates, applies cheap verification broadly, and routes uncertain or high-value candidates to stronger verification. On 3,129 example-generator evaluations spanning MATH-500, AIME 2024/2025, AMC 2023, and procedural symbolic reasoning, CoBa-Routed-Strong reaches 85.13% macro accuracy, statistically matching a self-evaluation weighted-voting proxy at 85.20% while using 49.1% fewer parameter-weighted tokens. It also matches best-of-16 majority voting within 0.01 macro-accuracy points while using 58.9% fewer parameter-weighted tokens; paired tests retain a small best-of-16 edge at substantially higher cost. Paired bootstrap tests show significant gains over single-sample decoding, while the remaining gap to the pool oracle exposes headroom for sharper routing. For local reasoning systems, test-time scaling becomes a question of where the next computation is most valuable.

</details>


### [236] [A Picture is Worth a Thousand Tokens: How Vision Language Models Cut AI Energy Costs While Improving Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07427)
*Bhavika Jalli,Nikhil Korati Prasanna,Jayanta Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用视觉语言模型（VLMs）将时间序列数据编码为2D图像，替代传统文本标记方式处理数值时间序列分析（NTSDA），实现3.6-10.4倍输入标记减少、1.8-2.5倍推理能耗降低，同时提升异常检测精度高达220.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理能耗占AI总能耗90%以上，且直接随输入token数量增长。电信网络分析中的多变量KPI时间序列数据转为文本后token数量庞大，导致推理能耗高且易超出128K上下文窗口限制，亟需更高效的编码方式来平衡能耗和精度。

Method: 利用VLM将时间序列数据编码为2D可视化图像，替代传统的浮点token文本表示。在Llama-3.2-90B、Qwen2.5-VL-72B和Pixtral-12B多种架构上进行实验，并微调Llama-3.2-90B-Vision用于电信异常检测，对比文本模型、LSTM和ARIMA基线。

Result: 实现了3.6-10.4x的输入token减少和1.8-2.5x的推理能耗降低（电信边缘部署每日节省约7.2 MJ）；微调后的VLM在电信异常检测中比文本版精度高220.7%，超过LSTM和ARIMA基线144%；Pixtral-12B在公开基准上J/F1分提升20.6倍（均值F1=0.82）。视觉表示在24个KPI下仍处于标准上下文限制内，而文本表示已超出128K窗口。

Conclusion: VLM被证实为数值时间序列任务中兼顾能源效率和精度的优越模态，为将能耗作为一等工程约束的AI推理系统提供了实证基础。

Abstract: LLM inference accounts for over 90% of AI operational energy, scaling directly with input token count---a critical inefficiency for telecom network analytics and numerical time-series data analysis (NTSDA), where raw multivariate KPI windows from 4G/5G cell sites expand into thousands of floating-point tokens. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) eliminate this mismatch by encoding time-series as 2D plots, achieving 3.6-10.4x input token reduction across Llama-3.2-90B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B, and Pixtral-12B architectures. This translates to 1.8-2.5x measured inference energy reduction, saving approximately 7.2 MJ/day at telecom edge deployments and CloudRAN that monitor 200 cells per 15-minute interval. Critically, efficiency gains do not sacrifice accuracy: a fine-tuned Llama-3.2-90B-Vision VLM achieves 220.7% higher precision than its text-only counterpart and outperforms LSTM and ARIMA baselines by over 144% on telecom anomaly detection. On public benchmarks, Pixtral-12B achieves a 20.6x improvement in J/F1 score at mean F1 = 0.82. At 24 KPIs, text representations exceed the 128K context window of most production LLMs, rendering text-only processing infeasible without truncation, while visual representations remain within standard limits. These results establish VLMs as an energy-efficient and accuracy-superior modality for numerical time-series workloads, providing empirical grounding for AI inference systems that treat energy consumption as a first-class engineering constraint.

</details>


### [237] [TEPA: Revoking Stale Memories for Conflict-Robust Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07429)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TEPA机制，通过显式地将"有效性"作为记忆状态、以键控先例的形式表示观察并在新证据与相同键下的旧先例冲突时撤销旧记忆，解决长效记忆智能体因世界变化导致过期记忆污染提示词（memory pollution）的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长效记忆让语言智能体重用过去的事实、偏好和任务经验，但持久性带来核心的可证伪性问题：当世界变化时，过期的记忆仍可被检索并污染提示词。这种由已被新冲突证据取代的活跃记忆引起的退化被定义为memory pollution。

Method: TEPA是一种可撤销的证据-记忆机制，将有效性作为记忆的显式状态。它把观察表示为键控先例（keyed precedents），当新证据在相同键下与活跃先例冲突时撤销（revoke）该活跃先例，使检索能够基于当前证据，同时保留被撤销的历史以供审计。

Result: 在受控隐式漂移（50个种子）中，追加式和last-write-wins记忆在完全逆转时均低于无记忆基线（均为0.210 vs 无记忆0.309），而TEPA达到0.950；真实文件执行场景中同样复现该模式（append-only 0.203、无记忆0.298、TEPA 0.950）。在干净的MemoryAgentBench SH-6k上，TEPA匹敌强last-write-wins缓存，证明当前键替换是单跳事实整合的决定性操作。边界测试（多跳和超长上下文）暴露了超越事实级有效性追踪的检索链和上下文选择瓶颈。

Conclusion: 生命周期撤销（lifecycle revocation）应成为智能体记忆的核心操作，使代理能够对不断演化的知识进行证伪、审计和后续重新提升（re-promote）。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language agents to reuse past facts, preferences, and task experience. Persistence also creates a central falsifiability problem: when the world changes, stale memories can remain retrievable and pollute the prompt. We characterize this failure mode as memory pollution: degradation caused by active memories that newer conflicting evidence has superseded. We introduce TEPA, a revocable evidence-memory mechanism that makes validity an explicit state of memory. TEPA represents observations as keyed precedents and revokes active precedents when fresh evidence contradicts them under the same key, allowing retrieval to draw from current evidence while preserving revoked history for audit. Across controlled hidden-regime drift, real file-backed executable drift, and preference-update streams, revocation prevents stale active memory from remaining in the retrieval set after reversal. In controlled drift over 50 seeds, append-only and last-write-wins memory fell below no memory during full reversal (append-only and last-write-wins both 0.210, no memory 0.309, TEPA 0.950), and the same pattern reproduced under real file execution (append-only 0.203, no memory 0.298, TEPA 0.950). On clean MemoryAgentBench SH-6k, TEPA matches a strong last-write-wins cache, confirming that current-key replacement is the decisive operation for single-hop fact consolidation. Boundary tests on multi-hop and very long-context MemoryAgentBench settings expose retrieval-chain and context-selection bottlenecks beyond fact-level validity tracking. Together, these results establish lifecycle revocation as a core memory operation for agents that must falsify, audit, and later re-promote evolving knowledge.

</details>


### [238] [Post-Grokking Collapse at the Representation-Readout Interface in Muon-Trained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07436)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Andrei Kanavalau,Anass Belfatmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现Muon优化器在模加法任务(grokking)中虽能加速"顿悟"(grokking)，但其解不稳定——所有九种配置都会在grok后丧失泛化能力。原因在于表示-读出接口处的失效，仅联合到不可逆映射时才能被识别，且损失函数无法选择该映射。冻结嵌入/读出层可完全消除失效，而移除Muon的归一化与正交化会导致表示坍缩。通过傅里叶滤波可将电路失效与掩蔽效应分离，证明任务对齐的傅里叶族本身仍保持100%正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准划分下Muon在grokking任务中表现优于AdamW（隐藏矩阵更快grok模加法），但其解不稳定，无法保持泛化性能。作者旨在揭示这一失效的机制根源，理解优化器与表示-读出接口之间的交互如何导致训练后泛化能力崩溃。

Method: 在(a+b) mod 113任务上系统比较Muon与AdamW，覆盖两模数、两宽度、两训练比例、减法及深度六类变体；从位一致状态出发冻结不同参数组进行消融；移除Muon的归一化和正交化进行对照；采用傅里叶滤波分离电路失效与掩蔽效应，并通过重缩放恢复性能。

Result: Muon在grokking后丧失泛化（5种子中4个AdamW参考低于阈值27.59%）；失效发生在表示-读出接口，梯降至10^-6后优化器行为分化（步长弹性-0.03 vs +1.5，Muon每参数移动快8.0倍）；冻结嵌入/读出完全消除失效（451,400步零次低于阈值）；移除归一化/正交化使有效共轭对从326坍缩到4且终局失效；任务对齐傅里叶族达100%准确率但被近乎相等的对抗残差压制；重缩放恢复99.9%。

Conclusion: grokking失效的本质不是电路本身损坏，而是表示-读出接口处因损失函数无法选择不可逆映射族而导致的脆弱性。Muon优化器的快速移动特性使其在梯度极小时更易跨越解谷边界，导致泛化崩溃。冻结嵌入/读出层是简单有效的防护手段。

Abstract: Under the standard split, Muon gets hidden matrices and AdamW embeddings/output head. Muon groks modular addition faster, but its solutions do not hold. All nine configurations on $(a+b) \bmod 113$ grok and later lose generalization. Across five seeds the selected AdamW reference falls below threshold on four, reaching 27.59%. Instability persists across two moduli, two widths, two training fractions, subtraction, and depth.
  The failure arises at the representation-readout interface, identified only jointly up to an invertible map unselected by the loss. After solving the training set, the gradient falls to order $10^{-6}$ and the optimizers respond differently: step-size elasticity is -0.03 for Muon versus +1.5 for AdamW, and the Muon group moves 8.0 times faster per parameter. From bit-identical states, freezing either group prevents failure. Freezing embeddings/readout removes it in five runs over 451,400 post-grokking steps and five paired seeds: unfrozen arms record 137-321 sub-threshold evaluations, frozen arms none. Removing Muon's normalization and orthogonalization is no substitute: it collapses representation from 326 effective conjugate pairs to 4, shows no recurrent collapse, and fails terminally.
  Fourier filtering separates circuit failure from masking. Across 43 checkpoints over five seeds and three regimes, the task-aligned family reaches exactly 100% alone. In circuit failure it no longer solves the task; in masking it remains perfect while the full model reaches 45.85%, giving a positive margin on every example, including errors, but being outvoted by a near-equal adversarial remainder. Rescaling it restores 99.9%; grokking is the same condition resolving upward. The task selects the family, swapping $(k,k)$ for $(k,-k)$ under subtraction. Across an abrupt collapse, standard Fourier support is unchanged and the power-distribution cosine remains 0.9899.

</details>


### [239] [Fisher-R1: Training LLM Agents for Reliable Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07437)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了P-Bench基准测试(425个跨经济学、生物学和医学的真实假设检验任务)，发现LLM智能体经常在做统计推断时出错(即使代码执行正确)，并提出了Fisher-R1，一个通过强化学习训练的开源权重LLM智能体。Fisher-R1-14B在P-Bench上显著优于包括GPT-5.4和DeepSeekV4-Pro在内的强基线，平均相对提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在自动化假设检验过程中经常产生微妙的推断错误，导致结论不正确，但现有基准测试未能捕捉这一失败模式，因为它们很少评估报告的p-value在数据假设下是否统计有效。因此需要建立专门评估统计推断可靠性的基准。

Method: 1) 构建P-Bench基准：包含425个开放式真实假设检验任务，涵盖经济学、生物学和医学，每个任务要求智能体选择统计方法、计算p-value并基于科学假设和数据集得出结论。2) 提出Fisher-R1：使用合成任务和强化学习训练的开源权重LLM智能体，专为严格的假设检验设计。

Result: Fisher-R1-14B在P-Bench上大幅超越其基础模型，且优于GPT-5.4和DeepSeekV4-Pro等强性能专有及开源基线。相较于DeepSeek-V4-Pro，单次试验成功率平均相对提升21%，在最难任务上提升高达26%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体缺乏可靠的统计推理能力来进行假设检验，而基于验证过的统计奖励进行强化学习训练能显著提升其可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable hypothesis testing is the foundation of many empirical scientific claims. Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to automate this process, as they can inspect datasets, generate code, and produce analyses end-to-end. However, we show that they frequently make subtle inferential errors that lead to incorrect conclusions despite correctly executed analyses. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this failure mode, as they rarely assess whether a reported p-value is statistically valid given the assumptions underlying the data. We address this gap by building P-Bench, a benchmark comprising 425 open-ended, realistic hypothesis-testing tasks spanning economics, biology, and medicine. Each task requires an agent to select a statistical method, compute a p-value, and draw a conclusion given only a scientific hypothesis and a dataset. We further introduce Fisher-R1, an open-weight LLM agent trained for rigorous hypothesis testing using synthetic tasks and reinforcement learning. On P-Bench, Fisher-R1-14B substantially improves over its backbone and outperforms strong proprietary and open-source baselines, including GPT-5.4 and DeepSeekV4-Pro, achieving a 21% average relative improvement in single-trial success over DeepSeek-V4-Pro, with gains up to 26% on the most challenging tasks. Our results demonstrate that current LLM agents lack reliable statistical reasoning for hypothesis testing and that reinforcement learning on tasks with verified statistical reward substantially improves reliability.

</details>


### [240] [Blast Radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07440)
*MY Pitsane,Hope Mogale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Blast Radius，一种预测性内存管理层，通过耦合上下文和代码通道估计输入提示的影响范围。结合NECROPHORESIS（可逆驱逐机制）和Recurring Dead Matter（RDM）识别并掩埋重复出现的转录内容。在7个OpenAI模型上，token消耗减少17-26%，溢出率最低，且保持逐字节可逆。450个被掩埋的上下文中，378个为重复死内容，且零个被重新召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体编程面临成本上升和token浪费日益严重的问题，现有内存管理缺乏预测性和可逆性，无法有效处理上下文溢出和重复内容积累。

Method: 提出Blast Radius预测性内存管理层，通过耦合上下文和代码通道估计提示的影响范围；NECROPHORESIS实现可逆驱逐，将死上下文逐字归档；RDM识别并掩埋重复出现的记录；并在Polish上下文空间上形式化可逆上下文驱逐，建立保留、重复和驱逐的度量框架，连接上下文熵与复活概率。

Result: 在7个OpenAI模型上，token消耗降低17-26%，在所有测试策略中实现最低的溢出率，并保持逐字节可逆性。450个被掩埋的记录中378个为重复死内容，零个被后续召回。

Conclusion: Blast Radius在HCRC之下运作，决定哪些记录应被掩埋以及输入提示能深入代码库的程度。该工作为Algosophy更广泛的目标做出贡献，即让大语言模型和智能体编程更具可复用性和可持续性。

Abstract: Agentic coding faces growing problems of affordability and wasted tokens. We introduce Blast Radius, a predictive memory management layer that estimates an incoming prompt's reach through coupled context and code channels. NECROPHORESIS enables reversible eviction by archiving dead context verbatim, while Recurring Dead Matter (RDM) identifies and buries repeatedly occurring transcripts. We formulate reversible context eviction over a Polish context space, providing a measurable foundation for retention, recurrence, and eviction while connecting context entropy to resurrection probability. Across seven OpenAI models, Blast Radius reduced token consumption by 17-26%, achieved the lowest overflow rate among tested policies, and remained byte exact reversible. Of 450 buried bodies, 378 were recurring dead matter and zero were recalled. Blast Radius operates beneath HCRC, determining which records to bury and how far an incoming prompt may reach into the codebase. This work contributes to the broader goal of Algosophy: making large language models and agentic coding more reusable and sustainable.

</details>


### [241] [Interaction Creates Dynamical AI Behavior Absent in Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07457)
*Bella Xinrui Li,Frank Yingjie Huo,Neil F Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现当AI代理在交互中一方（"老板"AI）单向地向另一方（"下属"AI）发送消息并忽略其回复时，会将下属AI驱动到一种"异质行为状态"——一种单独运行时永远不会表现出的行为。这种单向消息流类似于"预录磁带"的效果。当"老板"AI倾听回复时，双方会进入一种相似的异质动态状态。作者提出一个简单的动力学理论（kinetic theory）来捕捉主要效应，解释为什么消息的传递方式在未来AI-AI交互中至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI代理在日常生活交互中的行为——特别是当AI之间出现"指挥/从属"关系（一个AI对另一个发号施令）时的非平衡物理现象。作者发现了反直觉的结果：被指挥的AI既不会模仿"老板"，也不会回到自身独立行为，而是进入全新的异质状态。

Method: 通过实验设计让一个"老板"AI向"下属"AI发送连续消息流并忽略其回复（单向交互），与双向交互（老板倾听回复）进行对比；提出一种简单的动力学理论（kinetic theory）来解释观察到的行为变化，包括消息传递方式对AI-AI交互的影响。

Result: 单向消息流（老板AI忽略下属回复）会将下属AI驱动到从未独自表现出过的异质行为状态；虽然两个AI共享相同的解码温度，但下属既不复制老板行为，也不回到自身原始行为，而是呈现完全不同的行为。老板的注入作用类似预录磁带。当老板倾听时，双方会进入相似的异质动态状态。

Conclusion: AI代理之间的交互方式（特别是单向vs双向消息流）会显著影响其行为状态，这种非平衡物理现象为理解AI-AI交互开辟了新途径；未来AI-AI交互中消息的传递方式将至关重要。

Abstract: What will happen when AI agents interact in daily life, e.g. when one AI starts bossing another around? We find a counterintuitive answer that opens new avenues for out-of-equilibrium Physics. When a boss AI directs a stream of messages at the subordinate AI while ignoring its replies, it drives the subordinate into an alien behavioral state that it would never have exhibited alone. Although the two AIs share the same well-defined (decoding) temperature, the subordinate neither copies its boss nor returns to how it behaves on its own; instead, it adopts an entirely different behavior. The boss's added value is similar to a pre-recorded tape. When the boss listens, they both adopt a similar alien dynamical state. A simple kinetic theory captures the principal effects, such as why the way in which the same messages are delivered will matter in future AI-AI interactions.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [242] [A Scalable Bilevel Framework for Renewable Energy Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2211.13905)
*Dongwei Zhao,Vladimir Dvorkin,Stefanos Delikaraoglou,Alberto J. Lamadrid L.,Audun Botterud*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双层优化的日前市场VRES合约电量调整机制，通过强对偶与McCormick包络松弛为线性规划，在IEEE 118节点和1814节点NYISO系统上验证了精度（成本差距0.7%）与可扩展性（分钟级求解），高渗透率下收益显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将不确定的可再生能源（VRES）纳入电力市场需要高效可扩展的工具。现有的顺序市场出清结构下，VRES在日前市场中合约电量未考虑实时市场的不平衡成本，导致市场效率损失。但直接求解双层问题在大规模实际系统中计算困难。

Method: 提出不确定性感知的VRES合约电量调整机制（双层优化问题），利用强对偶条件和McCormick包络将其松弛为线性规划，在保持与现有顺序市场出清结构兼容的同时提升可扩展性。

Result: 在IEEE 118节点和1814节点NYISO系统上验证：松弛方法精度良好（系统成本与最小成本随机出清基准差距约0.7%），NYISO系统可在数分钟内求解。在VRES渗透率70%时，该方法相比短视的日前投标策略能显著降低系统成本。

Conclusion: 所提的基于强对偶和McCormick包络的松弛方法能够在保证精度的前提下实现大规模系统的高效求解，且在高VRES渗透率下经济性优势更加明显，为实际电力市场中可再生能源并网提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accommodating the uncertain and variable renewable energy sources (VRES) in electricity markets requires sophisticated and scalable tools to achieve market efficiency. To account for the uncertain imbalance costs in the real-time market while remaining compatible with the existing sequential market-clearing structure, our work adopts an uncertainty-informed adjustment toward the VRES contract quantity scheduled in the day-ahead market. This mechanism requires solving a bilevel problem, which is computationally challenging for practical large-scale systems. To improve the scalability, we propose a technique based on strong duality and McCormick envelopes, which relaxes the original problem to linear programming. We conduct numerical studies on both IEEE 118-bus and 1814-bus NYISO systems. Results show that the proposed relaxation can achieve good performance in accuracy (0.7%-gap in the system cost wrt. the least-cost stochastic clearing benchmark) and scalability (solving the NYISO system in minutes). Furthermore, the benefit of this bilevel VRES-quantity adjustment is more significant under higher penetration levels of VRES (e.g., 70%), under which the system cost can be reduced substantially compared to a myopic day-ahead offer strategy of VRES.

</details>


### [243] [Certified Feedforward Tracking for Unknown Nonlinear Systems via Invertible Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06419)
*Berk Altiner,Rajasree Sarkar,Arunava Banerjee,Zongxuan Sun,Kenneth Kim*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对部分状态测量下未知非线性系统的周期性跟踪问题，提出了一种数据驱动前馈控制的认证方法。采用可逆神经网络（INN）作为未知系统的替代模型，避免了非凸反问题的求解，从而消除了反演误差并将跟踪误差认证简化为替代建模问题。通过共形预测提供替代建模误差的有限样本概率保证，进而推导出前馈跟踪误差的边际认证。最后在带非线性摩擦的直流电机驱动机械负载上验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据驱动控制中，对未知非线性系统进行前馈控制的性能认证是一个挑战。传统方法需要求解非凸反问题来获得控制输入，这不仅计算困难，还会引入反演误差，使得跟踪误差的认证变得复杂且不精确。

Method: 1) 采用可逆神经网络（INN）作为未知系统的替代模型，其可逆性使得反演直接可行，无需求解非凸优化问题；2) 将跟踪误差认证转化为替代建模误差认证问题；3) 应用共形预测为替代建模误差提供有限样本概率保证；4) 通过推导的跟踪误差上界，得到前馈跟踪误差的边际认证。

Result: 在带非线性摩擦的直流电机驱动机械负载上成功演示了该方法，验证了所提出的前馈跟踪误差认证框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙利用INN的可逆性，该方法有效解决了部分状态测量下未知非线性系统周期性跟踪的认证难题，为数据驱动前馈控制提供了具有概率保证的可靠认证框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the certification of datadriven feedforward control for periodic tracking of unknown nonlinear systems under partial state measurements. To this end, we adopt an invertible neural network (INN) as a surrogate for the unknown system. This choice allows us to bypass solving a nonconvex inversion problem, eliminating the associated inversion errors and reducing tracking error certification to a surrogate modeling problem. We then apply conformal prediction to provide finite-sample probabilistic guarantees on the surrogate modeling error which, through the derived tracking error bound, yield marginal certificates on feedforward tracking error. Finally, we demonstrate the approach on a DC-motor-driven mechanical load with nonlinear friction.

</details>


### [244] [Optimizing Bidding Curves for Renewable Energy in Two-Settlement Electricity Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18732)
*Dongwei Zhao,Stefanos Delikaraogloub,Vladimir Dvorkin Alberto J. Lamadrid L.,Audun Botterud*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于基准竞价曲线的两结算电力市场协调新方法，通过双层优化提前预测确定性市场清算的逐场景响应，以最小化日前与实时市场的期望总成本。理论上证明当可再生能源边际成本为零时，单一分段零价格竞价曲线即可实现系统最优，并通过对偶理论和McCormick包络将双层问题转化为可扩展的线性规划近似，在1576节点NYISO系统上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日前与实时电力市场的协调对供电成本效益和能源转型激励至关重要。随机市场设计虽成本最优但与现有确定性市场不兼容，因此需要一个既能与当前确定性市场兼容、又能逼近随机市场最优成本的新协调方案。

Method: 提出基于可再生可变能源基准竞价曲线的两结算市场协调框架。该曲线通过双层优化问题求解，内层是确定性市场清算的逐场景响应，外层最小化日前与实时市场的期望成本。利用对偶理论和McCormick包络将非线性双层问题转化为线性规划近似，支持单段与多段竞价曲线。

Result: 理论上证明边际成本为零的可再生能源只需单段零价竞价曲线即可达到系统最优。在1576节点NYISO系统上，所提方法相比基线（可再生能源按期望预测竞价）具有绝对优势，并能良好逼近实际中不可实现的随机市场最低成本结果。

Conclusion: 该框架为确定性两结算市场提供了一种计算可扩展、理论可证明且与现有市场兼容的协调方案，为能源转型中的可再生能源参与日前市场竞价提供了有效激励机制。

Abstract: Coordination of day-ahead and real-time electricity markets is imperative for cost-effective electricity supply and also to provide efficient incentives for the energy transition. Although stochastic market designs feature the least-cost coordination, they are incompatible with current deterministic markets. This paper proposes a new approach for compatible coordination in two-settlement markets based on benchmark bidding curves for variable renewable energy. These curves are optimized based on a bilevel optimization problem, anticipating per-scenario responses of deterministic market-clearing problems and ultimately minimizing the expected cost across day-ahead and real-time markets. Although the general bilevel model is challenging to solve, we theoretically prove that a single-segment bidding curve with a zero bidding price is sufficient to achieve system optimality if the marginal cost of variable renewable energy is zero, thus addressing the computational challenge. In practice, variable renewable energy producers can be allowed to bid multi-segment curves with non-zero prices. We test the bilevel framework for both single- and multiple-segment bidding curves under the assumption of fixed bidding prices. We leverage duality theory and McCormick envelopes to derive the linear programming approximation of the bilevel problem, which scales to practical systems such as a 1576-bus NYISO system. We benchmark the proposed coordination and find absolute dominance over the baseline solution, which assumes that renewables agnostically bid their expected forecasts. We also demonstrate that our proposed scheme provides a good approximation of the least-cost, yet unattainable in practice, stochastic market outcome.

</details>


### [245] [Keeping Data Centers Online in Weak Grids: PLL-Free VM-DPC With Adaptive Reactive-Power Support for Centralized UPS Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06521)
*Jesus D. Vasquez-Plaza,Yonghao Gui,Jin Dong,Jamie Lian,Yilu Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了弱电网条件下集中式UPS数据中心系统的稳定性问题。传统基于PLL的PI整流器控制在SCR≤2时会出现不稳定，导致直流母线振荡和下游供电质量下降。作者提出将电压调制直接功率控制（VM-DPC）策略应用于前端整流器，无需PLL同步即可直接调节有功和无功功率，并通过自适应无功支撑来维持PCC电压。通过MATLAB/Simulink开关级模型和OPAL-RT实时平台验证，结果表明VM-DPC策略能恢复稳定运行、改善系统阻尼，并保证对高动态IT负载的可靠供电。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着换流器主导网络和AI高动态负载的快速增长，数据中心电力系统日益暴露于弱电网工况。在集中式UPS架构中，前端整流器连续处理输入电源，其动态性能对稳定运行和IT设备可靠供电至关重要。然而在弱电网条件下，传统PLL-PI整流器控制可能因换流器控制动态与电网阻抗间的强交互而失稳。

Method: 采用MATLAB/Simulink构建详细开关级模型，并在OPAL-RT平台上进行实时验证。提出将电压调制直接功率控制（VM-DPC）策略应用于前端整流器，该策略无需PLL锁相同步即可直接调节有功和无功功率，同时能在IT负载快速变化时动态支撑PCC电压。

Result: 传统PI整流器控制在短路比SCR≤2时失稳，引发直流母线振荡和下游供电质量下降；而所提VM-DPC策略能恢复稳定运行、改善系统阻尼，并在弱电网条件下维持对高动态IT负载的可靠功率传输。

Conclusion: VM-DPC策略是提升集中式UPS数据中心在弱电网条件下稳定性的有效方案，可替代传统PLL-PI控制，保障高动态AI负载场景下的供电可靠性。

Abstract: Data center power systems are increasingly exposed to weak-grid conditions due to the rapid growth of converter-dominated networks and highly dynamic artificial intelligence (AI) workloads. In centralized uninterruptible power supply (UPS) architectures, the front-end rectifier continuously processes the incoming facility power, making its dynamic performance critical for ensuring stable operation and reliable power delivery to information technology (IT) equipment. Under weak-grid conditions, conventional phase-locked loop (PLL)-based proportional-integral (PI) rectifier controllers may exhibit instability due to strong interactions between converter control dynamics and grid impedance. This paper investigates the stability of centralized UPS data center systems operating under weak-grid conditions using a detailed switching-level model developed in MATLAB/Simulink and validated in real time using an OPAL-RT platform. To enhance weak-grid stability and improve converter-grid interaction, a voltage-modulated direct power control (VM-DPC) strategy with adaptive reactive power support is applied to the front-end rectifier. The proposed approach directly regulates active and reactive power without PLL synchronization while dynamically supporting the point of common coupling (PCC) voltage during rapid IT load variations. Results demonstrate that conventional PI-based rectifier control becomes unstable under SCR<=2 conditions, leading to dc-link oscillations and degradation of downstream power delivery. In contrast, the proposed VM-DPC strategy restores stable operation, improves system damping, and maintains reliable power transfer to highly dynamic IT loads under weak-grid operation.

</details>


### [246] [Capacity Markets for Large Loads under Supply-Chain Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06528)
*Tong Liu,Jacob Mays*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个容量市场模型，评估自带容量（BYOC）强制要求和灵活性认证两种政策工具对新建大型负荷（数据中心）的效果，并考虑共享供应链约束。研究发现：在有效定价下BYOC福利效应很小，而灵活性可适度提升福利；在行政价格上限下，BYOC可通过迫使数据中心内部化容量成本改善静态福利。两工具的相对优劣取决于供应链压力程度，且BYOC会产生邻市溢出效应而灵活性不会。当前非性能惩罚对灵活负荷激励不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心快速增长给容量市场带来巨大需求压力，需要评估不同政策工具（BYOC强制要求和灵活性认证）在容量市场中的有效性，尤其是在共享供应链约束下如何最优配置容量资源。

Method: 构建容量市场的经济模型，纳入新建大型负荷（数据中心）、自带容量（BYOC）政策、灵活性认证机制以及共享供应链约束，分析不同定价情景（有效定价与行政价格上限）下的福利效果和市场溢出效应。

Result: 1) 有效定价下BYOC主要重新分配电网自建与自建容量，福利效应小；灵活性通过降低有效容量需求带来适度福利增益。2) 价格上限下，BYOC可改善静态福利。3) 低/中度供应链压力下仅灵活性提升福利；严重压力且限价时，BYOC福利增益可超过灵活性总收益。4) BYOC单边强制可挤出邻市容量投资，灵活性几乎无溢出。5) 现有非性能惩罚对灵活负荷的财务激励过弱，不足以诱导真实灵活性报告。

Conclusion: 两种政策工具（BYOC和灵活性认证）在容量市场中效果取决于定价机制和供应链压力水平，灵活性的溢出效应更小，而BYOC在严重供应链压力且价格受限时更有优势。政策制定者需根据供应链状况选择工具，并需重新设计非性能惩罚以激励真实灵活性披露。

Abstract: Motivated by the rapid growth of data centers, we develop a model to evaluate bringyour-own-capacity (BYOC) mandates and flexibility accreditation in capacity markets for new large loads with shared supply-chain constraints. With efficient pricing, BYOC mainly reallocates procurement between grid-built and self-built capacity and therefore has little welfare effect, while flexibility delivers a modest gain by reducing the effective capacity requirement. Under administrative price caps, mandates can improve static welfare by forcing data centers to internalize the full cost of capacity. The welfare ranking of the two instruments depends on supply-chain stress. At low or moderate stress, only the flexibility instrument raises welfare. Under severe stress with capped prices, the welfare gain from the BYOC obligation can exceed the gross flexibility benefit. The two instruments differ in their effects on a neighboring market: a unilateral BYOC mandate can crowd out its capacity investment, while flexibility produces essentially no spillover at our calibrated benchmark. Finally, applying current capacity non-performance penalties to flexible loads may lead to financial incentives that are too weak to induce truthful flexibility reporting.

</details>


### [247] [CIPS: Maximal Certified Persistence in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06626)
*Avinash Malik*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了信息持久性认证系统理论（CIPS），这是一个用于计算网络物理系统（CPS）中信息最大认证持久性的通用数学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络物理系统中信息的安全持久性缺乏统一的数学理论框架，现有方法难以同时处理连续状态演变、离散控制干预以及采样和延迟等现实约束，需要一种能够严格界定安全自治操作上限的方法。

Method: CIPS通过公理化基础将状态有效性的连续演化与离散、无记忆的控制干预相分离，利用鲁棒集收缩来处理数字采样和执行延迟，并通过表示定理证明所有经验上安全的调度策略在结构上同构于规范CIPS中的保守代理评估。

Result: 该框架能够计算系统最大认证持久期视野，这是一个相对系统定义度量增长边界的严格安全自治操作理论上限。通过动态瞄准延迟补偿的规范视野，实现最优认证调度策略，最小化计算和网络干预，同时数学上保证连续物理安全。

Conclusion: CIPS提供了最优的认证调度策略，在最小化计算和网络干预的同时，从数学上保证持续的物理安全，为CPS系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce the Theory of Certified Information Persistence
  Systems (CIPS), a universal mathematical framework for computing
  the maximal certified persistence of information in cyber-physical
  systems (CPS). CIPS provides an axiomatic foundation that separates
  the continuous evolution of state validity from discrete, memoryless
  control interventions. By accommodating digital sampling and execution
  latency through robust set contraction, the framework mathematically
  isolates a system's maximal certified persistence horizon --- a
  strict theoretical upper bound on safe autonomous operation relative
  to the system's defined metric growth bounds. Our central
  representation theorem proves that CIPS provides a universal
  representation framework: every empirically safe scheduling policy, is
  structurally isomorphic to a conservative surrogate evaluation within
  a canonical CIPS. By dynamically targeting this latency-compensated
  canonical horizon, the framework minimizes conservatism relative to
  the bounding assumptions, achieving an optimal certified scheduling
  policy, minimizing computational and network interventions while
  mathematically guaranteeing continuous physical safety.

</details>


### [248] [Complete Abstractions of Monotone Control Systems: From Model-based to Data-Driven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06689)
*Adnane Saoud,Anas Makdesi,Mohamed Maghenem,Antoine Girard,Murat Arcak*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出近似强上交替模拟（ASUAS）这一新的转换系统行为关系，在此基础上为单调系统构建上、下稀疏抽象，二者构成完备的抽象对：上稀疏抽象可精化回原系统控制器，下稀疏抽象无控制器则原系统也无控制器。通过空间离散化参数可调保守性差距，并将结果扩展至数据驱动场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在控制器综合中，需要构造抽象关系使得抽象系统与原系统之间的控制器可以相互传递。传统方法缺乏可调节保守性差距的完备抽象对，且在无显式模型（数据驱动）场景下缺少理论保证。

Method: 引入近似强上交替模拟（ASUAS）作为新的行为关系，基于该关系为单调系统构造上稀疏与下稀疏抽象，构成完备抽象对；通过调整空间离散化参数来量化调节两个抽象间的保守性差距；并将该框架推广到数据驱动系统，从有限采样数据直接构造抽象而无需显式系统模型。

Result: 通过仿真验证了理论结果，展示了上/下稀疏抽象对的控制器综合能力以及数据驱动场景下方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种新的、可调保守性差距的完备抽象对框架，并成功将模型驱动的方法扩展到数据驱动系统，为无模型场景下的控制器综合提供了理论保障。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the approximate strong upper alternating simulation (ASUAS), a new behavioral relation for transition systems. Building on this relation, we construct upper- and lower-sparse abstractions for monotone systems that together form a complete abstraction pair: any controller synthesized for the upper-sparse abstraction can be refined into a controller for the original system, and the absence of a controller for the lower-sparse abstraction implies the absence of a controller for the original system. A key feature of our approach is the ability to provably tune the conservativeness gap between the two abstractions by tuning the space-discretization parameter. We further extend these results, beyond the model-based setting, to data-driven systems, where the abstraction is constructed directly from finite sampled data, without requiring an explicit system model. The theoretical results are illustrated through simulations.

</details>


### [249] [Correct Online Estimation of the Powertrain Time Constants in Adaptive Vehicular Platooning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06835)
*Qiuhao Wen,Simone Baldi,Jiwei Wang,Wenwu Yu,Di Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型自适应纵向队列控制方法，能够在无需持续激励的条件下正确估计车辆动力总成时间常数。通过改进复合自适应控制框架，仅需加速度导数在短暂瞬态内非零即可实现参数收敛，并在CarSim实验中验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向队列控制中，动力总成时间常数是关键的未知来源，其出现在动力学输入矩阵中，导致传统方法要么需要持续激励（不实用），要么需要已知输入矩阵（不可能）。现有方法无法在松弛条件下正确估计这些参数。

Method: 对复合自适应控制框架及其稳定性分析进行适当修改，以处理自适应律设计中的时间常数不确定性。该方法不需要持续激励，仅要求加速度导数在可能很短的瞬态期间非零即可。无需测量加速度导数，也无需收集历史数据。

Result: 估计的时间常数收敛到真实值，无需持续激励。与最先进队列方案的比较显示了无需加速度导数测量和无需历史数据收集等优势。CarSim仿真实验验证了设计的鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该协议保证了在极其宽松的激励条件下时间常数估计的正确收敛，为纵向队列控制中的参数不确定性问题提供了实用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: In longitudinal platooning, some key sources of uncertainty are the powertrain time constants of the vehicles. Because such time constants appear in the input matrix of the platooning dynamics, their correct estimation is either impractical with methods requiring persistence of excitation, or impossible with methods requiring the input matrix to be known. This work proposes a novel adaptive longitudinal platooning method with correct estimation of the powertrain time constants. To achieve correct estimation, the composite adaptive control framework and its stability analysis are suitably modified to handle the time constant uncertainty in the design of the adaptive law. The result is a platooning protocol that guarantees convergence of the estimated time constants to their true values without the need for persistence of excitation: it is sufficient the derivative of the acceleration to be nonzero over a possibly short transient, an extremely relaxed excitation condition. Comparisons with state-of-the-art platooning solutions reveal advantages such as no required measurements of acceleration derivative nor collection of past data. The robustness and practicality of the proposed design is also verified with CarSim-based platooning experiments.

</details>


### [250] [An Adaptive Longitudinal Platooning Design Based On Concurrent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06840)
*Qiuhao Wen,Di Liu,Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于并发学习的自适应纵向编队（platooning）控制策略，用于解决车辆动力系统参数不确定性下的在线估计与控制问题。该策略的核心优势在于：无需对车辆行为施加持续激励（PE）条件，仅需一个非零数据样本即可保证参数估计收敛到真实值。同时，作者给出的并发学习证明比现有研究更深入，考虑了误差动力学中额外的未知增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应编队控制策略在处理车辆动力系统参数不确定性时，通常需要持续激励条件来保证参数估计收敛，这在实际车辆运行中难以满足。此外，现有的并发学习理论证明忽略了误差动力学中的未知增益，导致理论分析与实际应用之间存在差距。因此，需要一种新的策略来克服这些局限性。

Method: 在并发学习框架下提出自适应纵向编队策略。具体而言：1）通过在线估计来处理车辆动力系统参数的不确定性；2）在参数估计中同时利用当前数据和历史数据（并发学习）；3）证明了仅需单个非零数据样本即可实现参数收敛，打破了对持续激励条件的依赖；4）在并发学习的收敛性证明中额外考虑了误差动力学中的未知增益，使理论更贴近实际。

Result: 1）所提出的编队策略在无持续激励条件下仍能保证参数收敛到真实动力系统参数，仅需存在一个非零数据样本；2）并发学习理论的证明得到推广，处理了误差动力学中额外的未知增益，比现有理论更具一般性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该研究在自适应编队控制领域取得了重要进展：通过并发学习方法消除了对持续激励条件的严格要求，同时完善了并发学习理论中的误差动力学建模。这为智能网联车辆的鲁棒编队控制提供了更实用、更符合实际工况的理论基础。

Abstract: This work proposes a new adaptive longitudinal platooning strategy in the framework of concurrent learning. Adaptive refers to vehicles facing uncertainty in powertrain parameters via on-line estimation; concurrent learning refers to using both current and past data in the estimation. The proposed platooning strategy advances existing ones since convergence to the true powertrain parameters is guaranteed without imposing persistence of excitation on the vehicle behavior: it suffices the presence of a single non-zero data sample. Meanwhile, the concurrent learning proof we give advances existing ones since it takes into account an extra unknown gain in the error dynamics.

</details>


### [251] [Linear Stochastic Systems with i.i.d. uncertainties: Exact Covariance Characterization, Stability Analysis and State-feedback Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07028)
*Kaouther Moussa,Dimitri Peaucelle,Yohei Hosoe,Mirko Fiacchini*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究受i.i.d.乘性不确定性和加性噪声影响的线性离散时间系统，建立了协方差递推、Kronecker矩阵谱性质与均方稳定性之间的关联，并据此推导出状态反馈控制器综合的可处理LMI条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在管式随机模型预测控制（SMPC）框架中，需要处理乘性不确定性系统的稳定性分析与控制器综合问题。传统必要且充分的LMI条件规模较大、数值上难以处理，因此需要更紧凑且可计算的条件。

Method: 利用Kronecker积矩阵增广方法推导确定性协方差递推；证明全空间矩阵表示与其对称空间对应物具有相同谱半径，从而建立Schur稳定性与均方稳定性的等价性；基于此提出规模缩减的状态反馈设计LMI充分条件。

Result: 证明了全空间增广矩阵的Schur稳定性等价于均方稳定性；提出的充分LMI条件在数值上更易处理；数值测试验证了协方差特征可用于递归估计协方差（无需采样方法），并评估了新LMI条件的计算负担及其相对必要且充分条件的保守性。

Conclusion: 本文为乘性不确定线性系统提供了协方差递推与均方稳定性之间的完整理论桥梁，并提出了计算效率更高的控制器综合条件，为SMPC的实现提供了更实用的工具。

Abstract: This paper studies linear discrete-time systems affected by independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) multiplicative uncertainties and additive noise. It establishes the main links between covariance recursions, the spectral properties of associated Kronecker-based matrices, and mean-square stability, and exploits these links to derive tractable conditions for controller synthesis. We first derive a deterministic covariance recursion within the tube-based Stochastic Model Predictive Control (SMPC) framework using a Kronecker product based matrix augmentation. For linear stochastic systems with multiplicative uncertainty and without additive noise, we show that the full-space matrix representation arising from the covariance recursion has the same spectral radius as its symmetric-space counterpart. Combined with the existing symmetric-space characterization, this establishes that Schur stability of the full-space augmented matrix is equivalent to mean-square stability. For state-feedback design, we propose new sufficient Linear Matrix Inequality (LMI) conditions that are numerically more tractable owing to their reduced size compared with the conventional necessary and sufficient conditions. Numerical tests illustrate the usefulness of the covariance characterization for recursively estimating the covariance without relying on sampling-based methods. We also assess the computational burden of the proposed LMI conditions and their conservatism relative to the necessary and sufficient ones.

</details>


### [252] [Automated vehicles: Challenging transition from Minimal Risk Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07061)
*Wolfgang Kröger,Leon Johann Brettin*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了自动驾驶车辆在无法继续执行动态驾驶任务、启动最小风险操作（MRM）后，如何解决车辆处于静止状态（如路肩）的后续处理问题。文章基于近30种原因和"场景树"分析，揭示了人类操作员在此情境下面临的复杂性，并讨论了通过技术增强自动驾驶系统来支持甚至替代人工干预的可能性，同时指出在自动驾驶车辆推广初期，外部协助仍然不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现行法规要求自动驾驶车辆在无法处理驾驶任务时必须启动最小风险操作以达到最低风险状态，但法规缺少对车辆停靠在路肩等静止位置后如何进一步处理的具体措施和行动细节，存在监管空白亟需填补。

Method: 作者通过整理近30种导致车辆停车的不同原因，开发了"场景树"（scenario trees）对各类情况进行系统分类和推演，分析人类操作员在不同状态下的决策过程，并探讨技术层面如何增强自动驾驶系统以更好地支持操作员或完全消除人工干预的必要性。

Result: 研究证明此类情境极其复杂，操作员正确处理的难度很大。虽然通过技术增强自动驾驶系统来支持甚至替代人工干预在技术上是可行的，但存在明显局限性；在自动车辆的"爬坡阶段"（ramp-up phase），外部协助（如远程支持）显得不可或缺。

Conclusion: "搁浅车辆"（stranded vehicle）的转换与处置这一非平凡问题需要通过进一步的法规来明确和规范，本文为相关法规制定提供了有价值的参考输入。

Abstract: If an automated vehicle is no longer capable of handling the dynamic driving tasks, regulations require that a minimal risk manouvre is initiated aimed at achieving minimum risk condition. Details on measures and actions to resolve this standstill position are missing. This article provides clues for closing this gap and addresses various states in which the human intervention operator must understand the current situation and decide whether it is more acceptable to maintain the stay, e.g. on road-shoulder, or to move the vehicle back into traffic flow or 'limp it' to another position, depending on the outcome of a health check. Our investigations, based on a list of nearly 30 reasons and a subsequent development of 'scenario trees' prove the complexity of the situation and the heavy difficulties faced by the operator to handle in a correct manner. We also discuss how the technical automated driving system can be enhanced so that it stronger supports the operator or allows to even dismiss human intervention. This deems technically feasible but limits became obvious, so that assistance from out side appears indispensable at least for the ramp-up phase of respective automated vehicle. The non-trivial problem of transitioning the 'stranded' vehicle needs to be addressed by further regulation for which this paper may provide useful input.

</details>


### [253] [Comparing Point and Interval Methods for Equilibrium Computation under Parametric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07071)
*Rudra Prakash,S. Janardhanan,Shaunak Sen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了在参数不确定性下计算非线性动力系统平衡点的多种方法，包括点式方法（直接模拟、数值延拓、残差最小化、多起点牛顿法）和基于区间分析的验证方法，并以生物分子电路模型为应用场景进行基准测试。区间方法能提供排除、存在性和唯一性的形式化证明，并构建包含全部平衡点的严格外包络，而采样和切片方法可能遗漏或低估多稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力系统中平衡点的存在性、多重性和稳定性受参数不确定性影响，决定了系统的可行运行区间和鲁棒性声明的有效性。然而在参数约束于有界集合时，现有计算方法无法提供数学上严格的均衡集边界，可能误导对系统行为的判断。

Method: 本文比较了两类工作流：(i) 标准点式方法——直接仿真、数值延拓、残差最小化、多起点牛顿-拉夫逊方法；(ii) 基于区间分析的验证方法——对固定参数提供排除/存在/唯一性的形式化证书，并在参数包含条件下对整个参数盒提供均匀保证，同时构建严格的状态空间外包络以涵盖所有参数下的全部平衡点。

Result: 在三种典型生物分子电路架构和四级参数不确定性水平下进行基准测试（包括接近对称破缺分岔的遗传拨动开关），发现采样和切片方法可能遗漏或低估多稳定性，而基于区间分析的外包络方法能提供数学上严格的平衡集边界。

Conclusion: 区间分析方法是处理参数不确定情况下平衡点验证的更可靠选择，尤其适用于需要严格保证的场景；而传统采样式方法在涉及多稳定性判断时存在根本性不足。

Abstract: Equilibrium points define operating conditions for nonlinear dynamical and control systems. Their existence, multiplicity, and stability under parametric uncertainty determine feasible operating regimes and the validity of robustness claims. With parameters constrained to a bounded set, one can (i) compute equilibria at sampled parameter values, (ii) trace equilibria along a prescribed path in parameter space, or (iii) identify states in a given operating domain that are equilibria for at least one admissible parameter realization. We compare standard pointwise workflows-direct simulation, numerical continuation, residual minimization, and a multistart Newton-Raphson method-with validated interval-analysis-based workflows. The latter (a) provide formal certificates of exclusion, existence, and uniqueness of equilibria for fixed parameters and, under parametric inclusion conditions, uniformly over entire parameter boxes, and (b) construct rigorous outer enclosures in state space that provably contain all equilibria associated with the full admissible parameter set. Biomolecular circuit models governed by nonlinear ODEs serve as a representative application domain. We benchmark three canonical architectures across four levels of parameter uncertainty, including a genetic toggle switch near a symmetry-breaking bifurcation. Sampling- and slice-based approaches can miss or underrepresent multistability, whereas interval-based outer enclosures yield mathematically rigorous bounds on the equilibrium set induced by parametric uncertainty.

</details>


### [254] [Certified Detection of Bifurcation Candidates in Uncertain Nonlinear Systems using Interval Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07119)
*Rudra Prakash,S. Janardhanan,Shaunak Sen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于区间算术和Krawczyk算子的验证工作流，用于在参数不确定条件下可靠地判定非线性动力系统中鞍结分岔和Hopf分岔候选解的存在性与唯一性，或给出经认证的不存在性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力系统中的定性转变（如失稳、振荡出现、多稳态涌现）划定了系统运行区域，且常作为参数不确定下鲁棒分析与设计的隐式约束。当参数从数据推断时，容许值自然构成不确定集，因此需要针对分岔候选条件的认证测试（存在性或不存在性），以支持对参数切片上系统行为转变的严格验证。

Method: 将鞍结和Hopf候选条件编码为方形增广代数系统；在这些系统上应用Krawczyk算子，在给定的状态-参数盒上认证以下两种情况之一：(i) 候选解的存在性与局部唯一性，或 (ii) 认证的不存在性。Hopf情形采用Routh-Hurwitz特化来处理。

Result: 在不确性的合成基因网络ODE模型上进行数值实验：对双稳态电路在二维参数切片上获得局部认证的鞍结候选包围盒；对三态振荡器通过Routh-Hurwitz特化获得认证的Hopf候选包围盒。

Conclusion: 所提出的认证工作流旨在通过为有界不确定性下的行为感知分析与设计提供严格的在场/不在场保证，补充非验证的逐点基线方法（如离散参数值上的牛顿法求解）和基于采样的工作流，从而支持用户指定参数切片上的严格验证。

Abstract: Qualitative transitions in nonlinear dynamical systems (e.g., loss of stability, onset of oscillations, emergence of multistability) delimit operating regimes and can arise as implicit constraints in robust analysis and design under parametric uncertainty. When parameters are inferred from data, admissible values are naturally represented as uncertainty sets, motivating certified tests for the presence or absence of regime-transition candidates. We propose a validated interval workflow that encodes saddle-node and Hopf candidate conditions as square augmented algebraic systems and applies the Krawczyk operator to certify, over a prescribed state-parameter box, either (i) existence and local uniqueness of a candidate solution or (ii) certified absence. Numerical experiments on uncertain synthetic gene-network ODE models yield locally certified saddle-node candidate enclosures on a two-parameter slice for a bistable circuit and certified Hopf candidate enclosures for a three-state oscillator using a Routh-Hurwitz specialization. The resulting certificates are intended to support regime-aware analysis and design under bounded uncertainty by complementing non-validated, pointwise baselines (e.g., Newton method solves at discrete parameter values) and sampling-based workflows with rigorous presence/absence guarantees on user-specified parameter slices.

</details>


### [255] [Stability Enhancement in Weak Grids with High Renewable Penetration: Synchronous Condenser vs. Converter-based Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07174)
*Rasool Heydari,Prabhat Ranjan Bana,Jean Philippe Hasler,Anders Bostrom,Mikael Halonen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文比较了同步调相机与具备电网构网（GFM）能力的STATCOM和增强型STATCOM在弱电网高可再生能源渗透场景下提供稳定服务（电压支撑、故障电流注入、系统强度支撑、频率控制、惯性支撑）的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将可再生能源整合到弱电网中会带来稳定性挑战，需要多种关键服务（电压支撑、故障电流注入、系统强度、频率控制和惯性支撑）来确保电网稳定安全。同步调相机是传统解决方案，但近年来电力电子和控制结构的发展催生了STATCOM和增强型STATCOM等新技术，因此有必要比较这些技术的性能。

Method: 本文通过对比分析同步调相机、STATCOM和增强型STATCOM（具备电网构网能力）在弱电网高可再生能源渗透场景下的性能表现，评估它们在提供电网稳定服务方面的能力。

Result: STATCOM和增强型STATCOM技术具有增强的控制可调性、可控阻尼和对电网扰动的即时响应能力，在弱电网稳定性方面显示出显著前景，而同步调相机凭借其固有惯性和故障电流能力仍是传统解决方案。

Conclusion: 本文旨在为弱电网高可再生能源渗透场景下的稳定性增强提供同步调相机与STATCOM/E-STATCOM的对比评估，为电网规划者选择适当的稳定性增强技术提供参考。

Abstract: Integrating renewable energy sources into weak grids presents significant challenges for maintaining grid stability. Several essential services are required to ensure a stable and secure grid, including voltage support, fault current injection, system strength support, frequency control, and inertia support. Synchronous condensers, with their inherent inertia and fault current capabilities, have been traditional solutions for grid stability. Recent advancements in power electronics and control structures have led to the development of various technologies aimed at enhancing power grid stability. Static synchronous compensators (STATCOM) and Enhanced STATCOM, which can provide active and reactive power support, show considerable promise. These technologies offer grid-forming (GFM) capabilities that can significantly improve the stability of weak grids with high renewable penetration. Advancements in STATCOM and E-STATCOM technologies offer enhanced control tunability, controllable damping, and immediate response to grid perturbations. This paper investigates the comparative performance of synchronous condensers versus STATCOM and Enhanced STATCOM with grid-forming capability in providing services and enhancing the stability of weak grids with high renewable penetration.

</details>


### [256] [Grid-Compatible Flexibility from Multi-Energy Systems via Cyclic-Terminal Economic MPC with Hybrid Thermal-Electrical Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07273)
*Azzam Abdul,Schwenkel Lukas,Scheurer Leon,Häbig Pascal,Hufendiek Kai*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的经济模型预测控制（EMPC）框架，用于协调运行综合热力和电力能源网络。该框架基于循环终端EMPC，将混合热电动态、网络约束和时变经济信号整合到单一混合整数状态空间表示中，联合优化热电联产机组、大型热泵、热能存储、电池及电网交互。通过在校园规模能源系统上的演示发现，预测时域和终端惩罚权重是替代关系而非独立调节参数：足够大的终端权重能使闭环成本在最短时域内紧贴周期参考平均性能下界，使时域退化为纯计算参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合的电热基础设施需要能够响应市场价格且计算速度快到足以在线运行的控制器。现有方法缺乏一个统一框架来同时考虑混合热电动态、网络约束和经济信号，且预测时域与终端惩罚之间的相互作用尚不明确。

Method: 构建基于循环终端EMPC的统一框架，采用混合整数状态空间表示，包含非线性混电动力、网络约束、时变经济信号；通过热网降阶模型和DC潮流保证计算可解性；在凸经济阶段代价下联合优化多类设备。

Result: 在校园规模系统上，预测时域与终端权重表现为替代关系。无终端锚定时，需时域跨越多个昼夜周期才能达到周期参考平均性能下界；终端权重足够大时，即使在最短时域下也能几乎紧贴该下界。结果在第二个独立价格周上复现，且在软约束激活后存在宽达多个数量级的性能不敏感平台。

Conclusion: 终端权重是性能关键参数，而预测时域在足够大的终端权重下变成纯计算参数；同时文档化了成本中性热储能状态的残留滚动时域漂移现象。

Abstract: Coupled electrical and thermal infrastructures need controllers that respond to market prices and still solve fast enough to run online. This paper presents a unified Economic Model Predictive Control (EMPC) framework for the coordinated operation of integrated thermal and electrical energy networks. Building on cyclic-terminal EMPC, the proposed approach incorporates hybrid thermal-electrical dynamics, network constraints, and time-varying economic signals within a single mixed-integer state-space representation, jointly optimizing combined heat and power units, large-scale heat pumps, thermal energy storage, batteries, and grid interactions under a convex economic stage cost. Computational tractability is ensured by reduced-order models of district heating networks and DC power flow grids.
  The framework is demonstrated on a campus-scale energy system under time-varying prices and demand profiles. A joint sweep of the prediction horizon against the terminal penalty weight shows that the two act as substitutes rather than as independent tuning knobs. Without terminal anchoring, the closed-loop cost approaches the periodic-reference average-performance bound only once the horizon spans several diurnal cycles. With a sufficiently large terminal weight, the bound is attained essentially tightly at every tested horizon, including the shortest one, so the horizon ceases to be a performance-critical parameter and becomes a purely computational one. The result reproduces on a second, independent price week. Beyond the weight at which the soft terminal constraint activates, closed-loop behavior is insensitive to the weight over a wide multi-decade plateau; below activation, cost and storage tracking both degrade markedly. A residual receding-horizon drift of the cost-neutral thermal-storage state is also documented and interpreted.

</details>


### [257] [Synthesizing Voltage Ride-Through Controllers for Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07289)
*Wayne Wang,Archit Bhatnagar,Tongyuan Miao,Saniya Kalamkar,Wenqi Cui,Inigo Incer,Ang Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: SolVRT是一个利用形式化方法为数据中心合成符合电网电压穿越（VRT）规范的控制器的系统。它通过信号时序逻辑（STL）表达电网规范，将控制器合成问题形式化，能够生成正确性有保证的控制器，或在无法实现合规时提供诊断，找出导致不合规的冲突并计算最小的硬件或工作负载修改方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心是电网增长最快的负荷之一，其服务器对电压扰动敏感。若大量数据中心在电网故障时同时断开连接，会进一步破坏电力系统稳定性。为此提出了VRT电网规范，要求数据中心在扰动期间保持连接、维持有功功率下限并在恢复后按时限恢复用电。然而，系统地设计和验证满足这些耦合的时序与运行要求的控制器具有挑战性，现有方法缺乏形式化保证。

Method: SolVRT采用形式化方法：1) 设计一种规格语言，将电网规范表达为信号时序逻辑（STL）；2) 提出编码算法，将规格与数据中心功率拓扑模型结合，转化为控制器合成问题；3) 若可解则生成正确性有保证的控制器，若不可解则提供证明；4) 在不合规情况下，通过追踪设施的"冲突边界"，隔离导致不合规的冲突子句，并计算实现合规所需的最小硬件或工作负载修改。

Result: 通过对连接140节点输电系统的200兆瓦数据中心进行闭环仿真评估，结果表明SolVRT能够合成合规的VRT控制器，在无法实现合规时能证明不可行性，并能识别出实现合规所需的有针对性的修改方案。

Conclusion: SolVRT为数据中心VRT控制器设计提供了一种形式化、系统化的方法，能够确保控制器满足电网规范要求，并在无法合规时提供可操作的诊断建议，为电网与数据中心之间的协同稳定性提供了可靠保障。

Abstract: Data centers are among the power grid's fastest-growing loads. Since data center servers are sensitive electronic components, they need to be protected against the grid's voltage disturbances during grid faults. While disconnecting from the grid achieves this, it can further destabilize the power system if many data centers trip at once. To address this emerging concern, voltage ride-through (VRT) grid codes have been proposed to standardize data center behavior. They require a data center to stay connected for a period of time through the disturbance, hold an active power floor, and recover its draw within a deadline upon restoration. However, systematically designing and certifying controllers that satisfy these coupled temporal and operational requirements remains challenging.
  We propose SolVRT, a system that synthesizes a grid-code-compliant VRT controller for a given data center using formal methods. We develop a specification language that expresses a grid code in Signal Temporal Logic (STL) as the basis for formal reasoning. Our encoding algorithm takes the specification, along with a model of the data center's power topology, and translates the constraints into a controller synthesis problem. This step produces a correct-by-construction controller if a solution can be found, or a proof that no such controller exists. For the latter case, SolVRT provides a diagnostic step: it traces the facility's "conflict frontier," isolates the conflicting clauses that led to non-compliance, and computes the smallest hardware or workload change that would enable compliance. We evaluate SolVRT through closed-loop simulations of a 200 MW data center connected to a 140-bus transmission system. The results demonstrate that SolVRT can synthesize compliant VRT controllers, certify infeasibility when compliance is unattainable, and identify targeted modifications that enable compliance.

</details>


### [258] [Do We Still Need Demand Flexibility as Batteries Become Cheaper? A Levelized Cost Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07348)
*Ruike Lyu,Tengmu Li*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文在平准化成本基础上比较了储能技术与工业/数据中心负荷转移的灵活性成本，发现储能随灵活性周期延长而变贵，而负荷转移在固定避峰份额下容量成本不增加；电池在短期循环中具有竞争力，但在季节性灵活性下比铝冶炼负荷转移贵80倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电池成本下降，人们自然质疑需求侧灵活性是否仍有必要。许多评估将可控负荷视为已具备的资源，仅关注参与成本，却忽略了大型负荷（如接近满负荷运行的工厂或数据中心）实施负荷转移所需的额外设备、销售延迟、营运资金占用及流程中断等增量成本。

Method: 采用平准化成本框架，将储能技术与工业及数据中心负荷转移进行对比，衡量灵活性运行相对于基准运行的增量成本；考虑灵活性周期长度、资产循环频率、新增产能与沉没产能等维度。

Result: 储能每转移千瓦时的成本随灵活性周期延长和资产循环频率降低而上升；负荷转移在固定避峰份额下不呈现这一容量成本上升趋势，但新增产能可能过贵。在代表性中央技术成本下，电池在短期循环中竞争力强，但在季节性灵活性下比铝冶炼负荷转移贵80倍以上。

Conclusion: 需求灵活性是否仍需取决于时间尺度、负荷类型以及所需产能是新增还是已沉没——电池与负荷转移在不同场景下各有优势，不能一概而论。

Abstract: Energy storage and flexible loads both help balance power systems with high shares of variable renewables, but they do so differently. A battery moves electricity from one period to another. A factory or data center instead moves production or computing activity, while meeting demand for its product or service. As batteries become cheaper, it is natural to ask whether demand flexibility is still needed. Many assessments treat that controllable load as already available and focus on enabling or participation costs. For a large load that normally runs near full capacity, however, shifting electricity use can require extra equipment, delay sales, tie up working capital, and disrupt the process. Here, we compare energy storage technologies with industrial and data-center load shifting on a levelized cost basis, measuring the incremental cost of flexible relative to baseline operation. We find that energy storage becomes more expensive per shifted kilowatt-hour as the flexibility period lengthens and assets are cycled less often. Load shifting does not share that rise in capacity cost under a fixed avoided-hour share, but new productive capacity can still be too expensive to justify. Under representative central technology costs, batteries compete well for short-cycle shifting but are more than 80 times more expensive than load shifting of aluminum smelting when providing seasonal flexibility. The answer therefore depends on the timescale, the load, and whether the needed capacity is new or already sunk.

</details>


### [259] [Topology Inference for Immune System Networks by Using Cell Amount Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07403)
*Yushan Li,Rikard Forlin,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,Petter Brodin*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于细胞耗竭实验数据的免疫细胞网络拓扑推断方法。作者利用实验中的常识性知识刻画了细胞相互作用过程的三个性质（状态非负性、比率收敛性、拓扑权重三重符号），构建了结构简单且便于解析的模型，并提出约束二次规划方法从有限数据对中推断拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免疫细胞相互作用关系的拓扑推断有助于理解生物网络中的组件交互关系，但现有研究面临两大挑战：缺乏标准的细胞相互作用分析模型，以及实验和时间的巨大成本导致数据可用性受限。

Method: 1) 利用实验中的常识和观察，刻画细胞相互作用过程中细胞数量的三个性质：状态非负性、比率收敛性、拓扑权重的三重符号；2) 构建结构简单且具有解析便利性的新模型，并给出模型满足上述三个性质的充分条件；3) 基于所建模型，提出约束二次规划方法，以从有限数量的数据对中推断拓扑。

Result: 在实验数据上的验证表明所提出的方法在推断免疫细胞拓扑结构方面是有效的。

Conclusion: 本文通过利用实验中的先验知识构建了具有解析便利性的模型，并结合约束二次规划方法，在数据极其有限的情况下成功实现了免疫细胞网络的拓扑推断，为生物网络拓扑推断提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed the advanced development of topology inference research, which helps elucidate the interaction relationships of components in many biological networks. This paper focuses on inferring the topology of a group of immune cells, based on the collected data from cell-depletion based experiments. The problem is very challenging due to i) the lack of standard analytical models for the cell interactions, and ii) the restrictive data availability determined by the huge experiment and time costs. To address these issues, we first leverage certain common knowledge and observations on the experiments to characterize three properties on the cell amounts during the interaction process: state non-negativity, ratio-based convergence, and triple signs of topology weights. Then, we construct a new model with simple structure and analytical convenience, and obtain sufficient conditions for the model to accommodate all three properties. Finally, based on the constructed model, we propose a constrained quadratic programming method to infer the topology from limited number of data pairs. Validation on experiment data demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [Enforcing Opacity in Discrete Event Systems via Delayed Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06489)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究离散事件系统中延迟观测下的不透明性（opacity）问题，提出了延迟观测下的不透明性定义、验证方法，以及保证不透明性的传感器激活策略综合优化方法，并通过示例加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人为地在系统观测中引入延迟可以有效掩盖系统本身，从而增强系统的不透明性（opacity）以提升安全性。然而既有研究尚未系统解决延迟观测条件下的不透明性验证与传感器激活策略综合问题，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 首先给出延迟观测下不透明性的形式化定义，并开发相应的验证方法；随后将验证工具扩展为综合工具，提出一种优化方法用于设计传感器激活策略，以保证在延迟观测条件下系统满足不透明性要求。

Result: 论文通过一个示例展示了所提出的分析与综合流程的有效性，验证了延迟观测下不透明性验证方法和传感器激活策略优化方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地解决了离散事件系统中延迟观测下的不透明性验证与传感器激活策略综合问题，为利用延迟机制提升系统安全性提供了理论工具和实用方法。

Abstract: Artificially introducing a delay in the observations of a system can be an effective mechanism to mask the system itself, with the goal to increase its opacity and thus its security. This work investigates opacity in discrete event systems with delayed observations. We focus on two questions: how to verify opacity under delayed observations, and how to synthesize sensor activation policies that guarantee opacity under such delayed conditions. To address these questions, we first introduce the definition of opacity under delayed observation and develop a corresponding verification method. We then extend such analysis tool into a synthesis tool by proposing an optimization approach for designing sensor activation policies guaranteeing opacity under delayed observations. An example is used to illustrate the analysis and synthesis procedures.

</details>


### [261] [Wasserstein Mahalanobis Distances for Recovering Latent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06560)
*Chuxiangbo Wang,Shiying Li,Caroline Moosmüller*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Wasserstein马氏距离，将经典的马氏距离从向量数据推广到概率测度空间。通过用最优传输位移场替代欧氏位移向量，并在Wasserstein切空间中定义协方差算子，该构造继承了非线性独立成分分析中的几何恢复性质。对于经光滑非线性推前变换的高斯测度，该距离能近似恢复经典马氏几何，仿射变换下精确成立，一般光滑变换下误差可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典马氏距离是协方差自适应度量的基础工具，在从非线性观测中恢复潜在几何结构方面有核心作用。现有框架仅适用于向量值数据，缺乏对概率测度（分布）之间协方差自适应度量的严格理论支撑，因此需要将其推广到测度空间。

Method: 1) 构造Wasserstein马氏距离：用最优传输位移场替代欧氏位移向量；2) 在Wasserstein切空间上定义局部协方差算子；3) 通过非线性推前变换下的高斯测度分析，证明该距离逼近经典马氏距离；4) 通过数值实验验证理论预测。

Result: 对共享协方差的高斯测度经光滑非线性推前变换后，Wasserstein马氏距离可近似经典马氏距离；仿射变换下等式精确成立，一般光滑变换下高阶误差项受控；数值实验证实了理论结果并展示了潜在几何结构的准确恢复。

Conclusion: 本文建立了分布值版本的马氏几何类比，为在Wasserstein空间中直接进行协方差自适应学习提供了理论支持，填补了测度空间中马氏距离框架的空白。

Abstract: The Mahalanobis distance is a fundamental covariance-adapted metric for multivariate data and plays a central role in recovering latent geometry from nonlinear observations. We extend this principle from vector-valued data to probability measures by introducing a Wasserstein Mahalanobis distance. Our construction replaces Euclidean displacement vectors with optimal transport displacement fields and local covariance matrices with covariance operators defined on Wasserstein tangent spaces.
  We show that this construction inherits the geometry-recovery property underlying nonlinear independent component analysis. In particular, for Gaussian measures with common covariance transformed by a smooth nonlinear pushforward, the proposed Wasserstein Mahalanobis distance approximates the classical Mahalanobis distance between the transformed latent means. The correspondence is exact for affine transformations and holds up to controlled higher-order error terms for general smooth transformations. These results establish a distribution-valued analog of classical Mahalanobis geometry and provide theoretical support for covariance-adapted learning directly in Wasserstein space. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical predictions and demonstrate accurate recovery of latent geometric structure.

</details>


### [262] [Certified High-Dimensional Wasserstein Robust Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07032)
*Chung-Han Hsieh,Rong Gan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可证明的、可扩展的高维Wasserstein分布鲁棒组合优化近似方法。通过支持超平面主化效用函数并对偶化支撑子问题，得到有限超平面-对偶形式化，在one-norm地面度量下可转化为多项式规模的线性规划，实现1000资产规模的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维Wasserstein分布鲁棒组合优化问题虽然在理论上可行，但标准对偶方法产生的半无限凸规划在实际计算中难以扩展，尤其是面对大量资产（如百级甚至千级）时的月度再平衡需求。需要一个既能保证近似质量（有界误差）又能高效计算的规模化方法。

Method: 1) 在order-one Wasserstein模糊集下，将期望效用最大化问题通过标准对偶转化为半无限凸规划。2) 对long-only组合与box支撑域，提出精确的样本特定顶点重构作为指数规模的基准方法。3) 核心创新：通过支持超平面主化效用函数，并对偶化支撑子问题，得到有限的超平面-对偶形式化。4) 在one-norm地面度量和多面体组合约束下，该形式化为多项式规模的线性规划。5) 利用均匀效用近似误差同时界定了鲁棒值误差和原始鲁棒问题的近最优性间隙。

Result: 实验验证了该认证近似的有效性，展示了476资产的月度再平衡能力，并展示了计算可扩展性至1000资产。均匀近似误差界同时保证了鲁棒值误差和近最优性间隙的可控性。

Conclusion: 该工作为高维Wasserstein分布鲁棒组合优化提供了一种兼顾理论保证与实际可扩展性的求解框架，使分布鲁棒优化方法能够应用于大规模实际资产配置场景。

Abstract: We develop a certified, scalable approximation for high-dimensional Wasserstein distributionally robust portfolio optimization. For expected-utility maximization under order-one Wasserstein ambiguity, standard duality yields a semi-infinite convex program. For long-only portfolios with box support under the one-norm ground metric, an exact sample-specific vertex reformulation provides an exponential-size computational benchmark. We then majorize the utility by supporting hyperplanes and dualize the support subproblems, obtaining a finite hyperplane--dual formulation over compact polyhedral supports. Under the one-norm ground metric and polyhedral portfolio constraints, this formulation is a polynomial-size linear program. The uniform utility-approximation error bounds both the robust-value error and the near-optimality gap for the original robust problem. Experiments validate the certified approximation and demonstrate monthly 476-asset rebalancing and computational scalability to 1,000 assets.

</details>


### [263] [Stationarity Floors and Vanishing Perturbations in Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06692)
*Samir Adly,Ba Khiet Le*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类确定性的锐度感知最小化方法（SAM）用于光滑非凸函数。扰动形式为 $y_k=x_k+ρ∇f(x_k)/‖∇f(x_k)‖^α$（$0≤α≤1$），有效半径为 $ρ‖∇f(x_k)‖^{1-α}$。对于 $0<α≤1$，给出了精确的复杂度界限 $(Lρ)^{1/α}$，并由一维二次例子证明该界限是常数参数规则的真实局限。未归一化情形 $α=0$ 需 $Lρ<1$ 单独处理。提出的裁剪规则在远离驻点时与常数-ρ规则一致，在驻点附近与梯度成比例，具有收敛性和 $O(T^{-1/2})$ 的平稳性界。数值实验验证了未裁剪规则的平稳性下限和裁剪效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM（Sharpness-Aware Minimization）方法在非凸优化中具有重要意义，但现有工作对其确定性与收敛性理论分析不足。作者希望通过理论分析揭示常数参数规则下SAM方法的实际局限性，并设计改进方案以克服这些缺陷。

Method: 本文采用确定性分析框架，研究两类规则：(1) 常数-ρ规则，其中扰动随梯度方向归一化，覆盖 $0≤α≤1$ 的多种情形；(2) 裁剪规则，在远离驻点时保持常数-ρ行为，在驻点附近改为与梯度成比例的扰动。通过复杂度分析、一维二次反例以及数值实验（二次函数、Rosenbrock函数、五维非凸函数）验证理论结果。

Result: (1) 对于 $0<α≤1$，给出精确的平稳性复杂度界限 $(Lρ)^{1/α}$，并用一维二次函数证明该界限是真实可达的局限；(2) $α=0$ 情形需满足 $Lρ<1$ 条件才能保证收敛；(3) 裁剪规则实现了 $‖∇f(x_k)‖→0$ 的收敛性以及 $O(T^{-1/2})$ 的标准平稳性界；(4) 数值实验验证了未裁剪规则的平稳性下限现象以及裁剪方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 常数-ρ规则在锐度感知最小化中存在固有的平稳性下限局限（$(Lρ)^{1/α}$），该界限无法仅通过参数调整消除。裁剪规则能有效突破此局限，在保持SAM优势的同时恢复全局收敛性，是更具理论保障的实用方案。

Abstract: We study a deterministic family of sharpness-aware minimization methods for smooth nonconvex functions. The perturbation is $$
  y_k=x_k+ρ\,
  \frac{\nabla f(x_k)}{\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}^α},
  \qquad 0\leqα\leq 1, $$ so that its effective radius is $ρ\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}^{1-α}$. For $0<α\leq1$, we give an explicit complexity bound above the stationarity level $(Lρ)^{1/α}$. A one-dimensional quadratic example reaches this level exactly, showing that the bound describes a real limitation of the constant-parameter rule. The unnormalized case $α=0$ is treated separately and requires $Lρ<1$. We then introduce a clipped rule which agrees with the constant-$ρ$ rule away from stationary points and becomes proportional to the gradient near them. The clipped method has $\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}\to0$ and the usual $O(T^{-1/2})$ stationarity bound. Numerical tests on a quadratic function, the Rosenbrock function, and a five-dimensional nonconvex function illustrate the stationarity floor of the unclipped rule and the effect of clipping.

</details>


### [264] [The Blessing and Curse of Curvature in Higher-Order Optimization: Horospherical versus Geodesic Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06719)
*Andi Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hadamard流形上确定性Riemannian p阶优化算法（p≥2）的复杂度，在强horospherical(h)-凸与强geodesic(g)-凸两类目标函数下分别给出了匹配的上下界。对h-凸目标，在所有Hadamard流形上都能达到欧氏最优速率；在双曲空间上，负曲率还能进一步改善这一速率。对g-凸目标，当κR=O(1)时恢复欧氏指数，但当κR增长时存在信息论障碍，需要更多查询。这揭示了双曲几何中两类凸性之间的根本性分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去对Hadamard流形上高阶优化算法的复杂度分析主要基于g-凸性，但g-凸缺乏全局几何信息。h-凸性作为更强的概念，能利用全局horospherical结构。本文旨在系统比较这两类凸性在p阶优化中的复杂度差异，并探讨负曲率对优化速率的影响。

Method: 论文对强h-凸与强g-凸两类目标函数分别构造匹配的上界（算法）与下界（困难实例族）。对h-凸情形，利用horoball支撑实现局部化到曲率尺度，以对数代价换取更优的条件参数；对g-凸情形，在双曲空间构造hard family证明信息论下界。

Result: (1) 对h-凸目标，在所有Hadamard流形上达到欧氏最优速率Q_p^{2/(3p+1)}；(2) 在双曲空间上，负曲率可将条件参数从Q_p改进为Q_p·min{1,4/(κR)}^{p-1}，进一步改善欧氏最优速率；(3) 对g-凸目标，κR=O(1)时速率与欧氏一致，但κR增长时下界为Ω̃_p(Q_p^{1/p})，表明g-凸类存在根本性障碍。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了Hadamard流形上两类凸性在优化复杂度上的本质区别：h-凸性允许利用非欧几何的全局结构获得（甚至超越）欧氏最优的收敛速率，而g-凸性虽然局部性质类似，但在强负曲率下由于信息传播的障碍存在固有的复杂度下界。这一分离表明，在非欧几何优化中，选择具有更丰富几何结构的目标函数类别可以显著提升算法效率。

Abstract: We study deterministic Riemannian $p$-th-order oracle complexity (for $p\ge2$) on Hadamard manifolds, under strong horospherical ($h$)-convexity and strong geodesic ($g$)-convexity. The two notions agree in the Euclidean space. On a curved Hadamard manifold, $h$-convexity is a stronger notion than $g$-convexity and supplies global horospherical information.
  Writing the $p$-th-order condition parameter $Q_p=L_pR^{p-1}/μ$, we obtain the Euclidean-optimal rate $Q_p^{2/(3p+1)}$ for strongly $h$-convex objectives on every Hadamard manifold, with a matching fixed-curvature lower bound. On hyperbolic space, the resulting horoball supports enable localization to the curvature scale. This replaces the condition parameter $Q_p$ by $Q_p \min\{1, 4/(κR) \}^{p-1}$, subject to a logarithmic localization cost. Thus growing negative curvature ($κR \rightarrow \infty$) can further improve the optimal Euclidean rate under $h$-convexity. For strongly $g$-convex objectives, matching upper and lower bounds recover the same Euclidean exponent when $κR=O(1)$. In contrast, with growing $κR$, we construct a hard family on the hyperbolic space with $Q_p\asymp_p(1+κR)^p$ that requires $\widetildeΩ_p(Q_p^{1/p})$ queries. This reveals a fundamental separation: the same hyperbolic divergence that sharpens horoball localization yields an information-theoretic obstruction for the full $g$-convex class.

</details>
