### [265] [Connecting Extreme-Point Generation and Decision Rules in Two-Stage Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06821)
*Jin Qi,Chen Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过第二阶段对偶问题的极点将两阶段分布式鲁棒优化中的决策规则方法与分解生成方法联系起来。每个极点定义一个仿射价值片段，兼容的原始基可定义仿射策略片段。作者提出一种极端点生成方法求解第一阶段问题，并开发了后验精确性检验，同时扩展到退化、秩亏和结构化随机追索情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段分布式鲁棒优化中，决策规则方法需要预先指定自适应策略形式，而分解生成方法需要迭代构造第二阶段价值信息，两者缺乏统一的联系框架。

Method: 通过第二阶段对偶问题的极点建立两种方法的联系：每个极点定义仿射价值片段，兼容的原始基定义仿射策略片段；采用极端点生成方法求解第一阶段问题；通过单独的线性规划程序不断添加片段直至覆盖整个不确定集上的追索价值；基于算法输出开发后验精确性检验。

Result: 报告实例上算法计算高效；结果表明在不确定集上补全第二阶段价值所需的极端点数量多于仅求解第一阶段问题。

Conclusion: 论文建立了两种分布式鲁棒优化方法的统一理论框架，并提供了实用的算法与精确性检验工具，适用于退化、秩亏和结构化随机追索等扩展场景。

Abstract: Two-stage distributionally robust optimization chooses a here-and-now decision and a wait-and-see decision policy that adapts to uncertainty realizations. Decision-rule methods specify the form of this adaptive policy in advance, whereas decomposition-generation methods construct second-stage value information iteratively. We connect these approaches through extreme points of the second-stage dual problem. Each extreme point defines an affine value piece, and a compatible primal basis can define an affine policy piece. Solving the first-stage problem may require only a subset of these pieces. We specialize an extreme-point generation method to solve the first-stage problem. A separate linear-programming procedure adds pieces until it recovers the recourse value over the uncertainty set, and the pieces can also be used to recover the optimal recourse policy. Building on the algorithm output, we develop a posteriori exactness test for conventional decision rules. We give extensions for degeneracy, rank-deficient recourse, and structured random recourse. The proposed algorithm is computationally efficient on the reported instances, and the results show that completing the second-stage value across the uncertainty set requires more extreme points than solving the first-stage problem alone.

</details>


### [266] [Preference-Based Estimation of Achievement Scalarising Function Parameters via Ordinal Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06881)
*Maria Barbati,Salvatore Greco*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于序数回归的方法来估计多目标优化中成就标量化函数的参数（权重和参考点），并通过多目标背包问题的教学示例验证了该方法的灵活性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成就标量化函数在多目标优化中广泛应用，但其效果高度依赖于参数（权重和参考点）的恰当设定。如何从决策者的偏好信息中准确估计这些参数是一个具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 提出一种基于序数回归的方法，从决策者提供的偏好信息中推断成就标量化函数的两个关键参数：与目标相关的权重以及表征标量化函数的参考点。

Result: 通过一个基于多目标背包问题的教学示例展示方法，逐步说明偏好信息如何转化为模型参数，证明序数回归为校准成就标量化函数提供了灵活且可解释的框架。

Conclusion: 序数回归是支持决策者偏好诱导和成就标量化函数参数校准的有效框架，在多目标优化中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Estimating the parameters of an achievement scalarising function is a challenging task in multiobjective optimisation. Achievement scalarising functions are widely used in several contexts, including preference-based optimisation methods and algorithmic procedures for generating or selecting efficient solutions. However, their effectiveness strongly depends on the appropriate specification of their parameters, such as weights and reference points. In this paper, we propose an ordinal-regression-based methodology to estimate the parameters of an achievement scalarising function from preference information provided by the DM. In particular, the proposed approach allows us to infer both the weights associated with the objectives and the reference points characterising the scalarising function. The methodology is illustrated through a didactic example based on a multiobjective knapsack problem, which shows step by step how the preference information is translated into model parameters. The example illustrates that ordinal regression provides a flexible and interpretable framework for calibrating achievement scalarising functions and supporting the elicitation of decision-maker preferences in multiobjective optimisation.

</details>


### [267] [Stochastic Gradient Descent with Momentum: Analysis and Synthesis via Integral Quadratic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06915)
*Dennis Gramlich,Carsten W. Scherer,Christian Ebenbauer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用动态积分二次约束（IQCs）对加速随机梯度算法进行分析与综合。将小批量采样梯度建模为Lur'e系统中的反馈非线性，提出了一族刻画小批量梯度二阶统计特性的IQCs（扩展Zames-Falb乘子），并给出用于认证指数收敛率的半定规划及其对渐近方差进行界定的补充条件，最终提出一个凸综合程序以识别具有最小可认证收敛率的小批量梯度算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速随机梯度算法的收敛性分析通常面临两方面挑战：一是梯度噪声带来的随机性使传统确定性分析方法失效；二是现有分析工具难以同时处理收敛率认证与方差控制问题。本文动机是从鲁棒控制视角，利用IQC框架系统性地分析和综合加速随机梯度算法，以实现对随机性质量化和算法优化设计的统一处理。

Method: 本文方法包括三个部分：（1）提出一族刻画小批量梯度二阶统计量的IQCs，将经典Zames-Falb乘子推广到随机梯度场景；（2）构造半定规划（SDP）来认证加速随机梯度算法的指数收敛率，并补充一个条件用于界定由非消失梯度噪声引起的渐近方差；（3）提出凸综合程序，在可认证收敛率意义下寻找最优的小批量梯度算法参数。

Result: 本文实现了三个主要贡献：成功构建了针对小批量梯度二阶统计量的IQC族（推广了Zames-Falb乘子）；给出了一个可认证指数收敛率的半定规划及其补充的渐近方差上界条件；提出了一种凸合成方法，可识别出具有最小可认证收敛率的加速随机梯度算法。

Conclusion: 本文展示了鲁棒控制中的动态IQC框架可以有效应用于随机优化算法的分析与综合，为加速随机梯度算法的收敛率保证和方差控制提供了系统化的凸优化工具，并与经典控制理论（如Zames-Falb乘子）建立了新的联系。

Abstract: This article applies dynamic integral quadratic constraints (IQCs) to the analysis and synthesis of accelerated stochastic gradient algorithms. We consider composite objective functions whose gradient can be approximated via mini-batch sampling and we model the resulting stochastic gradient oracle as a feedback nonlinearity in the spirit of Lur'e systems from robust control. Our first main contribution is a family of IQCs that characterize the second-order statistics of mini-batch gradients, extending the classical Zames--Falb multipliers. Our second contribution is a semidefinite-program for certifying exponential convergence rates of stochastic gradient algorithms, and a complementary condition for bounding the asymptotic variance caused by non-vanishing gradient noise. Our third contribution is a convex synthesis procedure that identifies mini-batch gradient algorithms with the smallest certifiable convergence rate.

</details>


### [268] [Stability of Differential Stochastic Variational Inequalities with History-Dependent Responses and Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06923)
*Xiaojun Chen,Jian Guo,Xin Guo,Guan Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了一类微分随机变分不等式（DSVIs），将常微分方程（ODE）与历史依赖的随机变分不等式（SVI）耦合，建立闭环随机系统与含时变随机均衡的模型。在适当技术条件下证明了第二级响应的唯一性、可测性和Lipschitz连续性，进而保证状态轨迹的存在唯一性。基于样本路径的样本平均逼近（SAA）被证明一致收敛；还推导了参数变分不等式关于移动可行集的局部1/2-Hölder估计，以及以初始状态差和外生路径律的Wasserstein距离度量的轨迹稳定性界。数值实验和老年健康监测系统应用验证了方法的有效性，相似性加权复用预计算响应可达到接近全重算基准（0.97）的精度，同时将运行时间从86秒降至不到1秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机变分不等式与优化约束ODE的研究缺乏对历史依赖性和时变随机均衡的统一处理框架，且缺乏跨随机环境迁移学习的理论与计算支撑。本文旨在建立此类系统的严格理论基础并探索高效的迁移学习方法。

Method: 提出DSVI框架（ODE耦合历史依赖SVI）；建立响应函数的存在唯一性、可测性与Lipschitz连续性理论；构造基于独立样本路径的样本平均逼近（SAA）并证明均匀收敛；推导移动可行集参数变分不等式的局部1/2-Hölder估计及轨迹稳定性界；提出相似性加权复用预计算响应的算法以降低在线计算成本。

Result: 理论方面建立了DSVI解的存在唯一性、SAA一致收敛性及跨环境轨迹稳定性界。数值实验验证了SAA收敛与迁移稳定性；老年健康监测应用中，相似性加权复用达到接近0.97的基准精度，在线批量运行时间从86秒降至1秒以内；扰动与延迟更新实验刻画了鲁棒性及新鲜度-精度-计算成本的权衡。

Conclusion: 本文为历史依赖DSVI系统提供了完整的理论框架和高效迁移学习算法，兼具理论严谨性与实际计算效率，为相关闭环随机系统建模与部署提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and study a class of differential stochastic variational inequalities (DSVIs), in which an ordinary differential equation (ODE) is coupled with history-dependent stochastic variational inequalities (SVI). This framework models closed-loop stochastic systems with time-varying random equilibria and includes optimization-constrained ODEs as special cases. Under appropriate technical conditions, we establish uniqueness, measurability, and Lipschitz continuity with respect to the state of the second-stage response, and consequently the existence and uniqueness of the induced state trajectory.
  Moreover, we construct a sample average approximation (SAA) based on independent sample paths and prove uniform convergence of the approximate trajectories. For transfer between related stochastic environments, we derive a local $1/2$-Hölder estimate for parametric variational inequalities with moving feasible sets and a quantitative trajectory-stability bound in terms of the initial-state difference and the Wasserstein distance between exogenous path laws. Numerical experiments illustrate the SAA convergence and transfer-stability results. We further apply the framework to an elderly-health monitoring system. Similarity-weighted reuse of precomputed responses achieves an accuracy close to the full-recomputation benchmark of 0.97, while reducing the online batch runtime from 86 seconds to less than one second. Perturbation and delayed-update experiments additionally characterize robustness to sensor noise and the trade-off between response freshness, predictive accuracy, and computational cost. These results provide theoretical and computational support for efficient transfer learning in history-dependent DSVI systems.

</details>


### [269] [Free-Horizon Newton Method for Nonlinear Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07108)
*Gyeongrok Ha,Kenji Fujimoto,Ichiro Maruta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于非线性l1最优控制问题的轨迹优化方法，创新性地将控制时域作为自由变量，使其与l1范数输入共同被优化，从而同时实现能量效率和时间效率的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在固定控制时域下最小化输入的l1范数，无法同时评估控制持续时间和l1范数，限制了轨迹优化在时间与能量效率之间的权衡能力。

Method: 将控制输入和控制时域同时作为设计变量，扩展了基于牛顿的算法，利用关于这两个变量的梯度信息进行联合优化。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性：算法能够恢复航天器霍曼转移的解析解，并为地月转移确定最优控制时域。

Conclusion: 该方法无需预先指定控制时域即可找到高保真度的最优轨迹，实现了控制时域与l1范数输入的联合优化。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel trajectory optimization method for nonlinear l1-optimal control problems in which the control horizon is treated as a free variable, allowing both the control duration and the l1-norm of the control input to be evaluated. Unlike traditional approaches that minimize the l1-norm of the input under a fixed control horizon, the proposed method treats both the control input and control horizon as design variables, enabling joint optimization of energy and temporal efficiency. The method extends a Newton-based algorithm to accommodate this objective, leveraging gradient information with respect to both variables. This formulation enables one to find high-fidelity optimal trajectories without pre-specifying the control horizon. The effectiveness and robustness of the method are demonstrated through numerical simulations, in which the proposed algorithm recovers the analytical solution to the spacecraft Hohmann transfer, and determines an optimal control horizon for an Earth-Moon transfer.

</details>


### [270] [Establishing Boundary KKT Convergence of Mirror Descent through Reparameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07248)
*Kuangyu Ding,Kim-Chuan Toh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明镜像下降法在非凸问题中收敛到KKT点，且无需排除边界极限情况。收敛性通过在可定义边界扩展的度量展平重参数化S上应用KL论证来建立，并通过S的逆映射连续性恢复原始序列的KKT收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有镜像下降法在非凸问题中的收敛性分析通常需要排除边界极限情况，这限制了理论的适用性。作者希望建立更一般的收敛性保证，使结果在可验证条件下同时涵盖目标函数、Legendre核和可行域几何。

Method: 引入度量展平重参数化S，该映射具有可定义的边界扩展。将KL论证应用于重参数化后的目标函数，证明S(x_k)收敛，再利用S^{-1}的连续性恢复原始序列收敛到KKT点。

Result: 证明镜像下降在非凸问题中无需排除边界极限即可收敛到KKT点。将框架应用于Shannon熵、Fermi-Dirac熵和幂核等具体实例。

Conclusion: 未来工作可考虑更一般的约束几何和非可分离核，并将镜像下降扩展到更广泛的Bregman类方法，如Bregman近端点算法、Bregman ADMM及其非精确变体。

Abstract: We prove that mirror descent converges to a KKT point for the nonconvex problem without excluding boundary limits. The result holds under verifiable conditions that jointly couple the objective, the Legendre kernel, and the feasible geometry. The key ingredient to establish the convergence is a metric-flattening reparameterization \(S\) that admits a definable boundary extension. Applying the KL argument to the reparameterized objective yields convergence of \(S(x_k)\). Continuity of \(S^{-1}\) then recovers convergence to the KKT point of the original sequence. We further apply our general framework to some concrete examples: Shannon entropy, Fermi--Dirac entropy, and power kernels. Future work may consider more general constraint geometries and genuinely nonseparable kernels, and extend mirror descent to broader Bregman-type methods, e.g. Bregman proximal point algorithms and Bregman ADMM, and their inexact variants.

</details>


### [271] [Inverse reinforcement learning for indefinite mean-field social optimization with multiplicative noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07252)
*Ying Cao,Xun Li,Bing-Chang Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性二次型平均场社会优化中的逆强化学习问题，针对含乘性噪声和不定成本权重的系统，提出了基于模型和免模型的两种逆RL算法，从专家示范中恢复社会成本权重并复现最优控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题面临的挑战在于：系统含乘性噪声和不定成本权重，违反标准凸性假设；需要求解耦合随机代数Riccati方程和李雅普诺夫方程，且系统动力学未知，这对逆RL问题的求解带来了实质性困难。

Method: 1) 提出基于模型的逆RL算法：采用两个顺序循环分别处理个体和平均场动力学，证明了收敛性和闭环可镇定性，并刻画了恢复成本权重的非唯一性；2) 提出免模型的逆RL算法：结合积分RL和最小二乘辨识，仅需满足温和秩条件的实测轨迹数据，无需系统动力学信息。

Result: 数值仿真验证了所提两种算法的有效性，证明了在含乘性噪声和不定成本权重的平均场社会优化系统中，能够成功从专家示范中恢复成本权重并复现最优控制策略。

Conclusion: 本文为平均场社会优化中的逆RL问题提供了一套完整的理论框架和实用算法，同时克服了凸性假设缺失和系统动力学未知的双重困难，为该领域的后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the inverse reinforcement learning (RL) problem for linear-quadratic mean-field (MF) social optimization. The considered system features multiplicative noise and indefinite cost weights, which violate standard convexity assumptions and pose analytical challenges. The goal is to recover unknown social cost weights from expert demonstrations and reproduce the optimal control policies. This requires solving coupled stochastic algebraic Riccati equations and Lyapunov equations with unknown system dynamics. To this end, we first propose a model-based inverse RL algorithm with two sequential loops that separately handle individual and MF dynamics, and we prove its convergence and closed-loop stabilizability. Moreover, we characterize the non-uniqueness of the recovered cost weights. To eliminate reliance on system dynamics, we develop a model-free inverse RL algorithm using integral RL and least-squares identification, which requires only measured trajectory data satisfying mild rank conditions. Finally, numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the proposed approaches.

</details>


### [272] [Metric Non-Collapse in Learned World Models for Control: Approximation Theory, Finite-Sample Geometric Guarantees, and Deterministic Planning Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07265)
*Alain Bensoussan,Minh-Nhat Phung,Minh-Binh Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为非线性确定性控制系统的保度量学习的潜世界模型建立了三部分数学理论：近似理论、有限样本几何和确定性规划迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习世界模型在控制系统中需要保持度量结构以确保可靠规划，但缺乏严格的数学保证。本文旨在为非线性确定性控制系统中的度量保真学习潜世界模型提供完整的数学基础。

Method: 三部分理论框架：(1) 近似理论：构造光滑精确潜实现，验证范数约束张量积B样条类的有限容量C^{1,1}逼近性质；(2) 有限样本几何：引入仅编码器的局部-全局度量铰链损失，包含方向项和分离对项以防止无穷小塌缩和全局折叠；(3) 规划迁移：证明度量非塌缩产生兼容Lipschitz潜代价，半共轭给出均匀轨迹和有限时域代价界。

Result: 在正则可观测因子假设、低Ahlfors覆盖和均匀C^{1,1}预算下，当有限样本偏差低于度量边距阈值时，每个近似经验最小化器是逐点co-Lipschitz的，并满足均匀近似受控半共轭估计。L^2到L^∞的指数在Lipschitz正则性下是精确的。规划迁移部分给出优化器迁移保证。数值实验在有界潜范围、档案固定度量样本、系数盒样条代理和摆的潜模型预测控制上进行。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个端到端的严格数学框架，将近似误差、统计误差和训练优化误差分离，确保学习到的潜世界模型在度量意义上是保真的，并能可靠地迁移到确定性规划任务中。

Abstract: We develop a three-part mathematical theory for metrically faithful learned world models for nonlinear deterministic control systems. First, on the approximation-theoretic side, we construct smooth exact latent realizations and verify the required finite-capacity $C^{1,1}$ approximation property for norm-constrained tensor-product B-spline classes with capacity-independent regularity budgets. Second, on the finite-sample geometric side, we introduce an encoder-only local--global metric hinge whose directional and separated-pair terms prevent infinitesimal collapse and global folding. Although the prediction loss is observation-based, evaluation of this penalty uses state-metric supervision through observable-state distances and tangent directions. Under a regular observable-factor assumption, lower-Ahlfors coverage, and uniform $C^{1,1}$ budgets, provided the explicit finite-sample deviation lies below the metric-margin threshold, every approximate empirical minimizer above a computable one-sided regularization threshold is pointwise co-Lipschitz and satisfies a uniform approximate controlled-semiconjugacy estimate, with approximation, statistical, and training-optimization errors kept separate. The $L^2$-to-$L^\infty$ exponent used in this step is sharp under Lipschitz regularity. Third, for deterministic planning transfer, metric non-collapse induces compatible Lipschitz latent costs, while semiconjugacy gives uniform trajectory and finite-horizon cost bounds and an optimizer-transfer guarantee; learned latent cost heads enter through explicit compatibility errors. Numerical experiments use bounded latent ranges, archived fixed metric samples, a finite-capacity coefficient-box spline proxy motivated by the theorem, and latent model-predictive control on a controlled pendulum.

</details>


### [273] [On the Optimal Laplacian Jordan Structure for Synchronizability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07286)
*Luca Dieci,Cinzia Elia,Alessandro Pugliese*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究如何为具有给定图结构的微分方程网络选择Laplacian矩阵，以最大化网络的同步能力。作者针对三对角Laplacian类给出了满足三个最优标准的充要条件，并将结果推广到带状Laplacian，最后通过数值实验验证理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络同步研究中，Laplacian矩阵的特征值分布对同步性能至关重要。现有工作缺乏系统性的方法来选择最优的Laplacian结构，以使网络达到最佳的同步性能（包括稳定性、特征值散布最小化以及瞬态时间最短）。

Method: 作者提出了三个同步性能标准：(i) 确保同步解渐近稳定（通过主稳定函数为负衡量）；(ii) 最小化Laplacian特征值的归一化散布；(iii) 使瞬态尽可能短。在三对角Laplacian矩阵类中推导了满足这三条标准的充要条件，并将结果推广到带状Laplacian矩阵。

Result: 给出了三对角Laplacian矩阵满足三个同步性标准的充要条件，并成功将结果扩展到带状Laplacian情形。提供了大量数值实验来阐明理论结果，并与现有工作进行了比较。

Conclusion: 本文为微分方程网络中Laplacian矩阵的选择提供了系统性的理论指导，能够同时保证同步稳定性、最小化特征值散布和缩短瞬态时间。该工作为网络同步优化提供了重要的理论工具和实用方法。

Abstract: In this work, for a network of differential equations with a prescribed graph structure, our goal is to show how to select the Laplacian of the network in order to obtain the most favorable outcome insofar as synchronizability. That is, we will want to: (i) guarantee asymptotic stability of a synchronous solution (as measured by a negative value of the master stability function), (ii) minimize the normalized spread of the Laplacian eigenvalues, and (iii) have a transient as short as possible. Within the class of tridiagonal Laplacians, we give both necessary and sufficient conditions for satisfying our three criteria above, and give extension to banded Laplacians as well. Finally, we give extensive numerical results to elucidate our theoretical results and to compare to existing works.

</details>


### [274] [Sampling and Optimization meet Enhanced Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07329)
*Yuan Gao,Siming He,Eitan Tadmor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了两类具有快速收敛特性的动力学过程，其核心机制是利用传输-扩散动力学中的增强耗散效应来加速向Gibbs测度的收敛，并基于此设计了数值采样算法及相应的粒子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs概率测度的计算实现（采样与优化）在众多领域都至关重要，但传统方法的收敛速度往往不够理想。作者希望利用增强耗散机制来设计能够快速收敛到目标Gibbs测度的动力学过程。

Method: 论文引入两类传输-扩散动力学，其快速收敛由增强耗散（enhanced dissipation）驱动；基于这些动力学设计了面向目标Gibbs测度的数值采样算法，并进一步给出了对应的粒子系统。

Result: 提出了两类具有快速收敛性的动力学，并成功设计了相应的数值采样算法及粒子系统，可能为高效数值采样器提供新途径。

Conclusion: 通过增强耗散机制实现的传输-扩散动力学能够快速收敛到Gibbs测度，为采样和优化提供了新的理论和算法框架。

Abstract: It is well known that the computational realization of Gibbs probability measures, $e^{-\mathbb{U}(\mathbf{x})}/Z$, plays a central role in sampling and optimization. In this paper, we introduce two types of dynamics that exhibit rapid convergence towards these Gibbs measures. The mechanism driving this rapid convergence is the enhanced dissipation associated with these transport-diffusion dynamics. Motivated by these enhanced dynamics, we design numerical algorithms for sampling from the target Gibbs measure. Finally, we provide the corresponding particle systems that may yield other effective numerical samplers.

</details>


### [275] [Solving Monotone Linear-Quadratic Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problems via Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07336)
*Alberto Bemporad,Tatiana Tatarenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究N人广义纳什均衡问题（含凸二次成本与共享仿射约束），在伪梯度仅单调的条件下，证明求解变分广义纳什均衡（v-GNE）等价于求解一个由联合KKT条件导出的凸二次规划（QP），并提出加速近端点算法和加速投影梯度法，两者均达到O(1/k²)的近似v-GNE收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解单调线性二次v-GNE问题的方法效率有限，作者希望利用问题结构（凸二次成本+共享仿射约束），将v-GNE计算转化为凸QP，从而引入加速优化算法大幅提升求解效率。

Method: 1) 证明v-GNE与基于联合KKT条件构造的凸QP求解等价；2) 通过对该QP进行正则化，得到ε-近似v-GNE，次优性随正则化参数线性消失；3) 提出加速近端点方案和加速投影梯度法，两者在第k次迭代均达到O(1/k²)的近似v-GNE；4) 当博弈雅可比可逆时，可进一步降维到更低维度的QP求解。

Result: 理论分析与数值实验均表明，所提出的方法显著优于现有求解单调线性二次v-GNE问题的方案。

Conclusion: 通过将v-GNE问题转化为凸QP并结合加速算法，可以在一般单调假设下实现O(1/k²)的快速收敛，为单调线性二次广义纳什均衡问题提供了更高效的求解框架。

Abstract: We consider generalized Nash equilibrium problems among $N$ players with convex quadratic costs and shared affine constraints, assuming only that the game's pseudogradient is merely monotone. We show that computing a variational generalized Nash equilibrium (v-GNE) is equivalent to solving a single convex quadratic program (QP) derived from the players' joint Karush--Kuhn--Tucker conditions. Building on this, we show that the regularization of such a QP yields an $\varepsilon$-approximated v-GNE with suboptimality vanishing linearly in the regularization parameter. Next, we propose an accelerated proximal-point scheme and an accelerated projected-gradient method, both attaining an $\mathcal O(1/k^2)$-approximated v-GNE at the $k$-th iteration. We also demonstrate that an invertible Jacobian of the game allows for reduction to a lower-dimensional QP. Theoretical analysis and numerical experiments show the proposed methods substantially outperform the existing approaches to solve monotone linear-quadratic v-GNE problems.

</details>


### [276] [Uncovering expert objectives in production planning via inverse optimization: An industrial case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07398)
*Shivi Dixit,Rishabh Gupta,Adam Kelloway,John Wassick,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的逆向优化框架，从专家规划者的历史决策中推断隐含的目标函数。该方法将生产规划问题建模为混合整数线性规划，利用次优损失逆向优化方法学习目标权重，并在陶氏化学的实际工业案例中验证，发现避免库存短缺和维持一致周期长度是主导决策因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业生产规划常依赖优化模型，但定义合适的目标函数颇具挑战：规划者需平衡竞争目标、管理不确定性，并考虑难以量化的定性业务偏好，导致许多优化模型无法匹配专家行为，限制了信任度与实际采用率。

Method: 1) 将生产规划问题建模为混合整数线性规划(MILP)；2) 将未知目标函数表示为假设计算成本项的加权和；3) 采用基于次优损失(suboptimality-loss)的逆向优化方法，从历史生产计划中学习目标权重；4) 在陶氏化学的实际工业案例中应用，并引入时间和产品相关的扩展变体提升预测精度。

Result: 推断出的权重显示，避免库存短缺和维持一致的生产周期长度主导了规划者的决策；时间和产品相关的扩展进一步改善了预测精度，并揭示了决策优先级的演变；专家访谈证实了这些洞察的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 逆向优化能够将隐性专家经验转化为可解释的模型，从而为复杂工业系统提供更准确、更受信任的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Production planning in the manufacturing industry often relies on the use of optimization models, but defining an appropriate objective function can be a challenge. In practice, planners must balance competing goals, manage uncertainty, and account for qualitative business preferences that are difficult to quantify. As a result, many optimization models fail to match expert behavior, limiting trust and adoption. In this work, we propose a data-driven inverse optimization framework to infer the objective function implicitly captured in expert planners' decisions. We formulate the production planning problem as a mixed-integer linear program, where the unknown objective function is represented as a weighted sum of hypothesized cost terms. A suboptimality-loss-based inverse optimization method is then applied to learn the objective weights from historical production plans. The proposed approach is applied to a real industrial case provided by Dow, where the inferred weights reveal that avoiding inventory shortages and maintaining consistent cycle lengths dominate the planners' decision-making. Time- and product-dependent extensions further improve predictive accuracy and uncover evolving priorities. Expert interviews confirm the practical validity of these insights. Overall, this study shows that inverse optimization can transform tacit human expertise into interpretable models, enabling more accurate and trusted decision-support tools for complex industrial systems.

</details>


### [277] [A Domain-Specific Harness for End-to-End Automation of Optimization Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07407)
*Heechang Kim,Ernest K. Ryu,Shuvomoy Das Gupta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoOPT，一个用于优化研究端到端自动化的领域专用框架，将最优一阶方法的发现组织为四个阶段：基于BnB-PEP的数值设计、通过大语言模型进行符号发现与收敛证明、在Lean 4证明助手中进行形式化验证、以及人工解释与撰写。框架在两个案例中验证：lemniscate加速方法（实现梯度范数平方O(1/N⁴)的最优收敛率）和ITEM-f的解析描述（实现(1-√(μ/L))²的加速线性收缩率），两者的收敛定理均已在Lean 4中机器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化方法的研究传统上依赖人工推导和证明，过程繁琐且易出错。随着数值方法（如PEP）和LLM能力的进步，有必要建立一个系统化、端到端的自动化框架，将方法发现、证明、验证和撰写全流程整合，以加速优化研究并保证结果的可信度。

Method: AutoOPT采用四阶段流水线：(1) 通过BnB-PEP方法进行数值设计，发现最优方法的结构；(2) 利用前沿大语言模型进行符号化发现，推导解析描述和收敛性证明；(3) 在Lean 4证明助手中进行形式化机器验证，确保证明的正确性；(4) 由人类研究者进行解释和论文撰写。

Result: 在第一个案例中，lemniscate加速方法在N步梯度后以最优O(1/N⁴)速率降低光滑凸函数梯度范数平方，常数由经典椭圆积分常数lemniscate常数ϖ决定。在第二个案例中，此前仅数值已知的ITEM-f方法获得了解析描述，在L-光滑、μ-强凸最小化中以(1-√(μ/L))²的每步因子实现加速线性收缩。两个案例的收敛定理均在Lean 4中完成了形式化验证。

Conclusion: AutoOPT展示了将数值计算、LLM驱动推理和形式化证明相结合可以端到端地自动化优化研究的可行性。该框架不仅发现了新的最优方法（lemniscate加速），还为已有数值方法提供了机器验证的解析证明（ITEM-f），标志着优化方法研究向自动化、可验证方向迈出的重要一步。

Abstract: We present AutoOPT, a domain-specific harness for end-to-end automation of optimization research. AutoOPT organizes the discovery of optimal first-order methods into four stages: numerical design through the BnB-PEP methodology; symbolic discovery of the analytic description and a convergence proof through frontier large language models (LLMs); formal verification in the Lean 4 proof assistant; and human interpretation and write-up. We demonstrate the framework on two case studies, each of independent interest. The first, lemniscate acceleration, is a new accelerated gradient method for minimizing the gradient norm of a smooth convex function: after $N$ gradient steps it reduces the squared gradient norm at the optimal $O(1/N^{4})$ rate, with a constant governed by the lemniscate constant $\varpi$, a classical elliptic-integral constant. The second is the analytic description of ITEM-f, a method previously known only numerically: for $L$-smooth, $μ$-strongly convex minimization it contracts the function-value gap at an accelerated linear rate with a per-step factor $(1-\sqrt{μ/L})^{2}$. The convergence theorems of both case studies are formalized and machine-checked in Lean 4.

</details>


### [278] [Wasserstein Policy Gradient for Entropy-Regularized Linear-Quadratic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07433)
*Zhaoyu Zhu,Rui Gao,Shuang Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究熵正则化折扣线性二次（LQ）控制问题中的 Wasserstein 策略梯度（WPG）算法。通过 Bellman 验证论证表明，无约束问题的最优策略呈线性高斯形式，而 WPG 的梯度恰好与该策略类相切，从而将 WPG 精确约化为关于反馈增益和动作协方差的有限维常微分方程（ODE）。作者证明该 ODE 全局适定，并从任意可行初始点指数收敛；收敛指数在熵温度趋于零时存在正极限，且不含 exp(-c/τ) 这类扰动因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein 策略梯度（WPG）是一种通过在动作空间中传输概率质量来更新状态条件行动律的策略优化方法。以往对 WPG 收敛性的分析缺乏精确结论，尤其是在连续状态-动作空间以及熵正则化场景下。本文选择线性二次控制这一经典且可解析求解的框架，旨在精确刻画 WPG 的动力学行为和收敛特性，为理解更一般的最优传输型策略梯度方法提供理论基准。

Method: 1) 采用 Bellman 验证论证，证明熵正则化折扣 LQ 问题的最优策略为线性高斯策略；2) 分析以折扣占据权重加权的逐状态 Wasserstein 梯度，证明该梯度与线性高斯策略类相切，从而将无穷维策略优化精确约化为有限维 ODE；3) 通过常微分方程的定性理论（全局适定性、Lyapunov 型论证）分析反馈增益和协方差矩阵的动力学，证明指数收敛性；4) 对收敛指数进行温度渐近分析。

Result: 1) 证明了 WPG 在熵正则化折扣 LQ 问题中精确约化为有限维 ODE；2) 证明了该 ODE 从任意可行初始点出发全局适定且指数收敛；3) 收敛指数在熵温度 τ→0 时存在正极限，且不含有 exp(-c/τ) 形式的慢收敛因子（即无"温度阻碍"），仅保留对控制问题条件数的常规依赖。

Conclusion: 本文为 WPG 在 LQ 框架下的收敛性提供了精确且严格的刻画，表明熵正则化不会在低温极限下引入指数级的收敛瓶颈。这一结果揭示了 Wasserstein 策略梯度在结构良好的控制问题中的理论优势，也为后续将此类精确分析推广到非线性或非高斯场景奠定了基础。

Abstract: Wasserstein policy gradient (WPG) updates state-conditional action laws by transport in the action space. We study entropy-regularized discounted linear-quadratic (LQ) control. A Bellman verification argument shows that the unrestricted problem has a linear-Gaussian optimal policy, and the discounted-occupancy-weighted statewise Wasserstein gradient is tangent to this policy class. WPG therefore reduces exactly to a finite-dimensional ODE for the feedback gain and action covariance. We prove that this ODE is globally well posed and converges exponentially from every admissible initialization. For each fixed LQ problem, the exponent has a positive limit as the entropy temperature tends to zero and contains no perturbative factor of the form $\exp(-c/τ)$, while retaining the usual dependence on the conditioning of the control problem.

</details>
